@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "adaptive_sparse_attn",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "adaptive_sparse_attention",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
class AdaptiveSparseAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    supports_causal = True
    supports_bidirectional = True
    supports_gqa = True
    supports_rope = True
    triton_kernel: str = "sparse_attn"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        sparsity_ratio: float | None = None,
        dropout: float = 0.1,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        # Resolve hidden_size and num_heads safely even if config is None
        hidden_size = kwargs.get("d_model") or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or kwargs.get("dim")
        num_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_heads") or kwargs.get("n_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
        )
        num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") or kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads")

        head_dim = 0
        if config is not None:
            adapter = ModelAdapter(config)
            hidden_size = hidden_size or adapter.hidden_size
            num_heads = num_heads or adapter.num_heads
            num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads or adapter.num_key_value_heads
            head_dim = getattr(adapter, "head_dim", 0)

        if not hidden_size and source_attn is not None:
            cand = getattr(
                source_attn,
                "hidden_size",
                getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "embed_dim",
                    getattr(source_attn, "all_head_size", None),
                ),
            )
            if (
                cand is None
                and hasattr(source_attn, "q_proj")
                and hasattr(source_attn.q_proj, "in_features")
            ):
                cand = source_attn.q_proj.in_features
            hidden_size = cand

        hidden_size = int(hidden_size or 768)
        num_heads = int(num_heads or 12)
        num_kv_heads = int(num_kv_heads or num_heads)
        head_dim = head_dim if head_dim > 0 else (hidden_size // num_heads if num_heads > 0 else 64)

        self.hidden_size: int = hidden_size
        self.d_model: int = hidden_size
        self.num_heads: int = num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads: int = num_kv_heads
        self.head_dim: int = head_dim
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat: int = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        # Decoder & Causal detection
        model_type = getattr(config, "model_type", "").lower() if config else ""
        is_dec = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "is_decoder",
                getattr(config, "is_causal", kwargs.get("is_causal", False)),
            )
            if config
            else False
        )
        _mt = (model_type or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if (
            any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            or hasattr(config, "architectures")
            and any(
                "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal: bool = bool(is_dec)

        # Detect nested encoder architecture early
        source_cls = source_attn.__class__.__name__ if source_attn is not None else ""
        is_nested = source_cls in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
            source_cls.endswith(("SelfAttention", "SelfAttn"))
            and "distil" not in source_cls.lower()
        )
        has_out = (
            any(
                hasattr(source_attn, attr)
                for attr in (
                    "dense",
                    "out_proj",
                    "c_proj",
                    "out_lin",
                    "o_proj",
                    "wo",
                    "o",
                    "proj",
                )
            )
            if source_attn is not None
            else False
        )
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = bool(
            kwargs.get("is_nested_encoder", (is_nested and not has_out))
        )

        # Head dim fallback from source_attn if head_dim <= 0 or not resolved properly
        if source_attn is not None and getattr(source_attn, "head_dim", 0):
            head_dim = source_attn.head_dim
            self.head_dim = head_dim
            self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
            self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
            self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        # Default sparsity: lower for bidirectional encoders (0.1) than decoders (0.5)
        # Check explicit parameter, kwargs, comp_config, config
        cfg_encoder_sparsity = getattr(
            comp_config,
            "adaptive_sparse_encoder_sparsity",
            kwargs.get("adaptive_sparse_encoder_sparsity"),
        )
        if cfg_encoder_sparsity is None and config is not None:
            cfg_encoder_sparsity = getattr(config, "adaptive_sparse_encoder_sparsity", None)

        if sparsity_ratio is not None:
            effective_sparsity = float(sparsity_ratio)
        elif "sparsity_ratio" in kwargs:
            effective_sparsity = float(kwargs["sparsity_ratio"])
        elif comp_config is not None and hasattr(comp_config, "adaptive_sparsity_ratio"):
            effective_sparsity = float(comp_config.adaptive_sparsity_ratio)
        elif config is not None and hasattr(config, "adaptive_sparsity_ratio"):
            effective_sparsity = float(config.adaptive_sparsity_ratio)
        elif not self.is_decoder:
            if cfg_encoder_sparsity is not None:
                effective_sparsity = float(cfg_encoder_sparsity)
            else:
                effective_sparsity = 0.1
        else:
            effective_sparsity = 0.5

        self.default_sparsity: float = effective_sparsity

        # LoRA rank and alpha: independent reads
        if config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)),
            )
            self.lora_alpha = getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
        elif comp_config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank))
            self.lora_alpha = getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
        else:
            self.lora_rank = kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)
            self.lora_alpha = kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)
        # The user requested NOT to unfreeze the base weights.
        self.freeze_base: bool = self.lora_rank > 0

        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        out_lora_rank = 0 if self.is_nested_encoder else self.lora_rank
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=out_lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # Aliases
        self.q_lin = self.q_proj
        self.k_lin = self.k_proj
        self.v_lin = self.v_proj
        self.out_lin = self.out_proj

        # Per-head learnable sparsity logit initialized to target sparsity
        init_logit = math.log(
            max(1e-4, min(0.9999, effective_sparsity))
            / (1.0 - max(1e-4, min(0.9999, effective_sparsity)))
        )
        self.sparsity_logits = nn.Parameter(
            torch.full((self.num_heads,), fill_value=init_logit), requires_grad=True
        )
        self.output_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1))

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_dropout_prob",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", dropout),
                ),
            )
            if config
            else dropout
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)
        self.source_attn = None
        self.rotary_emb = (
            getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn is not None else None
        )

        self.has_relative_attention_bias: bool = False
        self.relative_attention_bias: nn.Embedding | None = None
        if config is not None:
            self.relative_attention_num_buckets: int = getattr(
                config, "relative_attention_num_buckets", 32
            )
            self.relative_attention_max_distance: int = getattr(
                config, "relative_attention_max_distance", 128
            )
        else:
            self.relative_attention_num_buckets = 32
            self.relative_attention_max_distance = 128

        self.triton_kwargs = {"block_size": 64, "eps": 1e-6}

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        """Freeze base projection weights when LoRA adapters are present."""
        for mod in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
            if hasattr(mod, "freeze_base_weights"):
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def unfreeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        """Unfreeze base projection weights."""
        for mod in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
            if hasattr(mod, "unfreeze_base_weights"):
                mod.unfreeze_base_weights()

    @property
    def query(self) -> nn.Module:
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self) -> nn.Module:
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self) -> nn.Module:
        return self.v_proj

    def _relative_position_bucket(
        self,
        relative_position: torch.Tensor,
        bidirectional: bool = True,
        num_buckets: int = 32,
        max_distance: int = 128,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        relative_buckets = 0
        if bidirectional:
            num_buckets //= 2
            relative_buckets += (relative_position > 0).to(torch.long) * num_buckets
            relative_position = torch.abs(relative_position)
        else:
            relative_position = -torch.min(relative_position, torch.zeros_like(relative_position))
        max_exact = num_buckets // 2
        is_small = relative_position < max_exact
        relative_position_if_large = max_exact + (
            torch.log(relative_position.float() / max_exact)
            / math.log(max_distance / max_exact)
            * (num_buckets - max_exact)
        ).to(torch.long)
        relative_position_if_large = torch.min(
            relative_position_if_large,
            torch.full_like(relative_position_if_large, num_buckets - 1),
        )
        relative_buckets += torch.where(is_small, relative_position, relative_position_if_large)
        return relative_buckets

    def compute_bias(
        self, query_length: int, key_length: int, device: torch.device | None = None
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        if self.relative_attention_bias is not None:
            context_position = torch.arange(query_length, dtype=torch.long, device=device)[:, None]
            memory_position = torch.arange(key_length, dtype=torch.long, device=device)[None, :]
            relative_position = memory_position - context_position
            relative_position_bucket = self._relative_position_bucket(
                relative_position,
                bidirectional=(not self.is_decoder),
                num_buckets=self.relative_attention_num_buckets,
                max_distance=self.relative_attention_max_distance,
            )
            values = self.relative_attention_bias(relative_position_bucket)
            values = values.permute([2, 0, 1]).unsqueeze(0)
            return values
        return torch.zeros((1, self.num_heads, query_length, key_length), device=device)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. HuggingFace / standard UniversalAdapter extraction
        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 2. GPT-2 Fused c_attn / c_proj (handling Conv1D and LoRALinear wrapper)
        if (
            hasattr(src, "c_attn")
            and src.c_attn is not None
            and (
                extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
            )
        ):
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if weight is not None:
                weight = weight.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (weight.ndim == 2 and weight.shape[0] < weight.shape[1])
                ):
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                bias_data = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    weight[:split_size, :],
                    bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    (bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None),
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    weight[2 * split_size :, :],
                    bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (w_o.ndim == 2 and w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1])
                ):
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (
                    w_o,
                    bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. RoBERTa / BERT Nested Encoder handling
        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            dense = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (
                dense.weight.data.clone(),
                dense.bias.data.clone() if dense.bias is not None else None,
            )

        # 4. Modern Decoders (Qwen3.5, LLaMA, Mistral) & DistilBERT
        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        if hasattr(layer, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                layer.weight.data.clone(),
                                getattr(layer, "bias", None),
                            )
                            break

        # Detect nested encoder architecture
        cls_name = getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, attr)
            for attr in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        self.is_nested_encoder = (
            cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention")
            or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
            or (hasattr(src, "query") and not has_out_proj)
            or (hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src, "output"))
        ) and not has_out_proj

        # Check required weights
        missing = [k for k in ["q", "k", "v"] if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"AdaptiveSparseAttention missing required weights for projections: {missing}"
            )

        # Frobenius norm validation on slices
        for proj_key, target_mod in [
            ("q", self.q_proj),
            ("k", self.k_proj),
            ("v", self.v_proj),
        ]:
            w = extracted[proj_key][0]
            if w is not None:
                out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
                norm = float(torch.norm(w[:out_d, :in_d].float()).item())
                if norm < 1e-4:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        f"AdaptiveSparseAttention extracted weight for {proj_key} has near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm:.6f})"
                    )

        if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
            w_o = extracted["o"][0]
            out_d = min(self.out_proj.weight.size(0), w_o.size(0))
            in_d = min(self.out_proj.weight.size(1), w_o.size(1))
            norm_o = float(torch.norm(w_o[:out_d, :in_d].float()).item())
            if norm_o < 1e-4:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"AdaptiveSparseAttention extracted weight for output projection has near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm_o:.6f})"
                )

        # Copy into projection modules
        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in [
                ("q", self.q_proj),
                ("k", self.k_proj),
                ("v", self.v_proj),
                ("o", self.out_proj),
            ]:
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    target_w = target.weight
                    out_d, in_d = target_w.shape
                    src_out, src_in = w.shape
                    target_w.data[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)].copy_(
                        w[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)]
                    )
                    if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                        b_val = b.data if isinstance(b, nn.Parameter) else b
                        target.bias.data[: min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                            b_val[: min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))]
                        )
                elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    min_d = min(target.weight.shape[0], target.weight.shape[1])
                    target.weight.data.zero_()
                    target.weight.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    if target.bias is not None:
                        target.bias.data.zero_()

        if hasattr(src, "rotary_emb") and src.rotary_emb is not None:
            self.rotary_emb = src.rotary_emb

        if hasattr(src, "has_relative_attention_bias"):
            self.has_relative_attention_bias = bool(src.has_relative_attention_bias)
        if hasattr(src, "relative_attention_bias") and src.relative_attention_bias is not None:
            self.relative_attention_bias = src.relative_attention_bias
            self.has_relative_attention_bias = True
        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 or self.freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    def get_sparsity_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        r"""Regularization loss encouraging head sparsity."""
        return torch.sigmoid(self.sparsity_logits).mean()

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        key_value_states = kwargs.get("key_value_states", encoder_hidden_states)
        is_cross_attention = key_value_states is not None
        kv_input = key_value_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states

        B, N_q, D = hidden_states.shape
        N_k = kv_input.shape[1]
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # Apply RoPE (accept from position_embeddings or fallback to rotary_emb)
        pos_emb = (
            position_embeddings
            if position_embeddings is not None
            else kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        )
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) >= 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            rotary_mod = self.rotary_emb
            pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
            if pos_ids is None:
                pos_ids = torch.arange(N_q, device=device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
            # 1. Try rotary_module(Q, position_ids)
            # 2. Try rotary_module(Q, seq_len=...)
            # 3. Try rotary_module(K, ...)
            cos, sin = None, None
            for probe_t in (Q, K):
                try:
                    res = rotary_mod(probe_t, pos_ids)
                    if isinstance(res, (tuple, list)) and len(res) >= 2:
                        cos, sin = res[0], res[1]
                        break
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning("RoPE embedding failed on (probe_t, pos_ids): %s", e)
                try:
                    res = rotary_mod(probe_t, seq_len=N_k)
                    if isinstance(res, (tuple, list)) and len(res) >= 2:
                        cos, sin = res[0], res[1]
                        break
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning("RoPE embedding failed on (probe_t, seq_len): %s", e)
                try:
                    res = rotary_mod(probe_t)
                    if isinstance(res, (tuple, list)) and len(res) >= 2:
                        cos, sin = res[0], res[1]
                        break
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning("RoPE embedding failed on (probe_t): %s", e)
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        chunk_size = 64
        scale = d**-0.5

        # Handle T5 position bias
        position_bias = kwargs.get("position_bias")
        if position_bias is None and self.has_relative_attention_bias:
            position_bias = self.compute_bias(N_q, N_k, device=device)

        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")

        is_decoder = (
            self.is_decoder
            or getattr(self.config, "is_decoder", False)
            or getattr(self.config, "model_type", "")
            in ("gpt2", "qwen", "qwen2", "qwen3_5", "llama")
        )

        sparsity_per_head = torch.sigmoid(self.sparsity_logits).clamp(0.0, 0.95)  # [H]
        k_float = (1.0 - sparsity_per_head) * N_k
        k_floor = torch.clamp(k_float.floor().long(), min=1, max=N_k)
        k_ceil = torch.clamp(k_floor + 1, min=1, max=N_k)
        alpha_k = (k_float - k_floor.float()).view(1, H, 1, 1)  # [1, H, 1, 1]
        
        k_indices_floor = (k_floor - 1).view(1, H, 1, 1)
        k_indices_ceil = (k_ceil - 1).view(1, H, 1, 1)
        valid_m = None
        if mask is not None and mask.dim() != 4:
            valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N_k, device)
            if valid_m is None:
                valid_m = torch.ones(B, N_k, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

        context_chunks = []
        attn_chunks = [] if output_attentions else None

        for q_start in range(0, N_q, chunk_size):
            q_end = min(q_start + chunk_size, N_q)
            q_chunk = Q[:, :, q_start:q_end, :]  # [B, H, C_q, d]
            C_q = q_end - q_start

            scores_chunk = (
                torch.matmul(q_chunk, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            )  # [B, H, C_q, N_k]

            if position_bias is not None:
                pb_chunk = position_bias[:, :, q_start:q_end, :]
                if mask is not None and mask.dim() == 4:
                    pb_chunk = pb_chunk + mask[:, :, q_start:q_end, :].to(scores_chunk.dtype)
                scores_chunk = scores_chunk + pb_chunk.to(scores_chunk.dtype)
            elif mask is not None:
                if mask.dim() == 4:
                    scores_chunk = scores_chunk + mask[:, :, q_start:q_end, :].to(
                        scores_chunk.dtype
                    )
                elif valid_m is not None:
                    scores_chunk = scores_chunk.masked_fill(
                        (~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0
                    )

            if is_decoder:
                q_idx = torch.arange(q_start, q_end, device=device).view(1, 1, C_q, 1)
                k_idx = torch.arange(N_k, device=device).view(1, 1, 1, N_k)
                causal_chunk = k_idx <= q_idx
                scores_chunk = scores_chunk.masked_fill(~causal_chunk, -10000.0)

            # Vectorized Top-K Thresholding on chunk with differentiable soft sigmoid threshold
            if not is_cross_attention:
                sorted_scores, _ = torch.sort(scores_chunk, dim=-1, descending=True)
                k_idx_f = k_indices_floor.expand(B, H, C_q, 1)
                k_idx_c = k_indices_ceil.expand(B, H, C_q, 1)
                thresh_f = torch.gather(sorted_scores, dim=-1, index=k_idx_f)
                thresh_c = torch.gather(sorted_scores, dim=-1, index=k_idx_c)
                topk_thresh = thresh_f * (1.0 - alpha_k) + thresh_c * alpha_k
                temperature = 0.1
                soft_mask = torch.sigmoid((scores_chunk - topk_thresh) / temperature)
                scores_chunk = scores_chunk + torch.log(soft_mask + 1e-8)

            attn_chunk = torch.nan_to_num(F.softmax(scores_chunk.float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0).to(
                dtype
            )
            attn_chunk = self.dropout(attn_chunk)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                    hm = head_mask[:, :, q_start:q_end, :]
                else:
                    hm = head_mask
                attn_chunk = attn_chunk * hm

            ctx_chunk = torch.matmul(attn_chunk, V)  # [B, H, C_q, d]
            context_chunks.append(ctx_chunk)
            if output_attentions:
                attn_chunks.append(attn_chunk)

        context = torch.cat(context_chunks, dim=2)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N_q, self.all_head_size)
        attn = torch.cat(attn_chunks, dim=2) if output_attentions else None

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(context)

        if position_bias is not None:
            return output * self.output_scale, position_bias
        return output * self.output_scale, (attn if output_attentions else None)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def compute_bias(self, query_length: int, key_length: int, device: torch.device | None = None) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def compute_bias(
    self, query_length: int, key_length: int, device: torch.device | None = None
) -> torch.Tensor:
    if self.relative_attention_bias is not None:
        context_position = torch.arange(query_length, dtype=torch.long, device=device)[:, None]
        memory_position = torch.arange(key_length, dtype=torch.long, device=device)[None, :]
        relative_position = memory_position - context_position
        relative_position_bucket = self._relative_position_bucket(
            relative_position,
            bidirectional=(not self.is_decoder),
            num_buckets=self.relative_attention_num_buckets,
            max_distance=self.relative_attention_max_distance,
        )
        values = self.relative_attention_bias(relative_position_bucket)
        values = values.permute([2, 0, 1]).unsqueeze(0)
        return values
    return torch.zeros((1, self.num_heads, query_length, key_length), device=device)

def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
    """Freeze base projection weights when LoRA adapters are present."""
    for mod in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
        if hasattr(mod, "freeze_base_weights"):
            mod.freeze_base_weights()

class Any (*args, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class Any(metaclass=_AnyMeta):
    """Special type indicating an unconstrained type.

    - Any is compatible with every type.
    - Any assumed to have all methods.
    - All values assumed to be instances of Any.

    Note that all the above statements are true from the point of view of
    static type checkers. At runtime, Any should not be used with instance
    checks.
    """

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if cls is Any:
            raise TypeError("Any cannot be instantiated")
        return super().__new__(cls)

Special type indicating an unconstrained type.

  • Any is compatible with every type.
  • Any assumed to have all methods.
  • All values assumed to be instances of Any.

Note that all the above statements are true from the point of view of static type checkers. At runtime, Any should not be used with instance checks.

Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "bam_umoe", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True
)
class BAMUMoEBlock(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int = 64,
        num_experts: int = 2,
        top_k: int = 1,
        layer_id: int | None = None,
        num_layers: int | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        moe_enabled: bool = False,  # BAM-FIX-02
        svd_rank: int | None = None,  # BAM-FIX-03
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        # BAM-FIX-07: config None-safe
        if config is None:

            class _Cfg:
                pass

            config = _Cfg()
            config.hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
            config.num_attention_heads = kwargs.get(
                "num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
            )
            config.head_dim = kwargs.get("head_dim", 64)
            config.model_type = kwargs.get("model_type", "")

        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        self.dim = int(
            kwargs.get("hidden_size")
            or getattr(
                config,
                "hidden_size",
                getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "dim", 768)),
            )
        )
        self.hidden_size = self.dim
        self.num_heads = int(
            kwargs.get("num_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            or getattr(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                getattr(config, "num_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 12)),
            )
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", self.num_heads),
            )
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))
        self.kv_repeat = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads

        cfg_hd = getattr(config, "head_dim", None)
        self.head_dim = int(
            cfg_hd or kwargs.get("head_dim") or (self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads))
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.lora_rank = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                (
                    getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", lora_rank)
                    if comp_config is not None
                    else lora_rank
                ),
            )
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                (
                    getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha)
                    if comp_config is not None
                    else lora_alpha
                ),
            )
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # BAM-FIX-03: SVD rank for real compression
        default_svd = min(128, self.dim)
        self.svd_rank = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "svd_rank",
                (
                    getattr(comp_config, "svd_rank", svd_rank)
                    if comp_config is not None
                    else (svd_rank or default_svd)
                ),
            )
        )

        # Decoder detection
        mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
        is_dec = bool(getattr(config, "is_decoder", False) or getattr(config, "causal", False))
        if any(
            mt.startswith(p)
            for p in (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        if old_attn is not None and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.num_experts = max(1, int(num_experts))
        self.top_k = min(max(1, int(top_k)), self.num_experts)
        self.moe_enabled = bool(moe_enabled)

        # ---- Q/K/V/O projections ----
        # BAM-FIX-03: use FactorisedLoRALinear with svd_rank
        # If svd_rank >= min(dim, all_head_size), degenerates to dense (no compression)
        Q_rank = min(self.svd_rank, self.dim, self.all_head_size)
        KV_rank = min(self.svd_rank, self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size)
        O_rank = min(self.svd_rank, self.all_head_size, self.dim)

        self.q_lin = FactorisedLoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            Q_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.k_lin = FactorisedLoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            KV_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.v_lin = FactorisedLoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            KV_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.out_lin = FactorisedLoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            O_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )

        # ---- Optional MoE ----
        if self.moe_enabled:
            self.qkv_moe: UnifiedMoE | None = UnifiedMoE(
                self.dim,
                num_experts=self.num_experts,
                top_k=self.top_k,
                output_dim=self.all_kv_head_size,  # MoE refines V (matches V dim)
                gate_init=kwargs.get("moe_gate_init", -3.0),
            )
        else:
            self.qkv_moe = None

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self.layer_id = layer_id
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.comp_config = comp_config
        self.rotary_emb = None

        # BAM-FIX-09: storage accounting
        self._orig_storage_bytes = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes = 0

        if old_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(old_attn)

    # ---- aliases ----
    @property
    def q_proj(self):
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def k_proj(self):
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def v_proj(self):
        return self.v_lin

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_lin

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def out_proj(self):
        return self.out_lin

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            return

        try:
            for p in pretrained_attn.parameters():
                self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        cls_name = pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = any(
            hasattr(pretrained_attn, a)
            for a in ("out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj", "out_proj", "dense")
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb") and pretrained_attn.rotary_emb is not None:
            self.rotary_emb = pretrained_attn.rotary_emb

        extracted = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn") and pretrained_attn.c_attn is not None:
            c = pretrained_attn.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
            cw = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
            if cw is not None:
                cw = cw.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (cw.ndim == 2 and cw.shape[0] < cw.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    cw = cw.t().contiguous()
                split = cw.size(0) // 3
                bmod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c, "bias", None))
                cb = bmod.data.clone() if bmod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (cw[:split], cb[:split] if cb is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (
                    cw[split : 2 * split],
                    cb[split : 2 * split] if cb is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    cw[2 * split :],
                    cb[2 * split :] if cb is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_proj") and pretrained_attn.c_proj is not None:
                o = pretrained_attn.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
                ow = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
                if ow is not None:
                    ow = ow.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (ow.ndim == 2 and ow.shape[0] < ow.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        ow = ow.t().contiguous()
                    bo = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(o, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (ow, bo.data.clone() if bo is not None else None)

        # Nested BERT / RoBERTa
        elif hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query"):
            s = pretrained_attn.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                    extracted[key] = (
                        m.weight.data.clone(),
                        b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense"):
                d = pretrained_attn.output.dense
                b = getattr(d, "bias", None)
                extracted["o"] = (
                    d.weight.data.clone(),
                    b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                )

        # Flat modules
        else:
            candidates = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in candidates.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(pretrained_attn, n):
                        m = getattr(pretrained_attn, n)
                        if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                            extracted[key] = (
                                m.weight.data.clone(),
                                b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(pretrained_attn, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"BAMUMoE.copy_weights: missing {missing_after} from "
                f"{pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__}"
            )

        def _orient(w, in_dim):
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_lin,
            "k": self.k_lin,
            "v": self.v_lin,
            "o": self.out_lin,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        # Identity for nested encoder bypass
                        eye = torch.eye(
                            target.out_features,
                            target.in_features,
                            device=target.U.device,
                            dtype=target.U.dtype,
                        )
                        target.set_svd_factors(eye, None)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

                # Orient to (out, in)
                in_dim = self.all_head_size if proj_key == "o" else self.dim
                w = _orient(w, in_dim)

                # BAM-FIX-08: shape tolerance
                out_d = min(target.out_features, w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target.in_features, w.size(1))
                if (out_d, in_d) != (target.out_features, target.in_features):
                    padded = torch.zeros(
                        target.out_features,
                        target.in_features,
                        device=w.device,
                        dtype=w.dtype,
                    )
                    padded[:out_d, :in_d] = w[:out_d, :in_d]
                    w = padded

                # Handle GQA pooling for K/V
                if proj_key in ("k", "v"):
                    src_out = w.shape[0]
                    tgt_out = target.out_features
                    if src_out > tgt_out and src_out % tgt_out == 0:
                        rep = src_out // tgt_out
                        w = w.view(tgt_out, rep, -1).mean(dim=1)
                    elif src_out < tgt_out and tgt_out % src_out == 0:
                        rep = tgt_out // src_out
                        w = w.repeat_interleave(rep, dim=0)

                target.set_svd_factors(w, b)
                target.freeze_base_weights()

        # Track compressed bytes
        try:
            for m in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
                for p in m.parameters():
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def get_aux_loss(self, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
        """BAM-FIX-04: MoE aux loss for training loop."""
        if self.qkv_moe is None:
            return torch.zeros((), device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        return self.qkv_moe.get_aux_loss(device)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        dtype_in = hidden_states.dtype

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        S = kv_input.shape[1]

        q = (
            self.q_lin(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        k = (
            self.k_lin(kv_input)
            .view(B, S, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.v_lin(kv_input)
            .view(B, S, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # ---- RoPE ----
        cos = kwargs.get("cos")
        sin = kwargs.get("sin")
        pos_emb = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) == 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]

        if cos is not None and sin is not None and not is_cross:
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))

        # ---- GQA ----
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        # ---- MoE refinement (only self-attn, only if enabled) ----
        if self.qkv_moe is not None and not is_cross:
            moe_delta_v = self.qkv_moe(hidden_states)
            moe_delta_v = moe_delta_v.view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            moe_delta_v = moe_delta_v.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
            if self.kv_repeat > 1:
                moe_delta_v = repeat_kv(moe_delta_v, self.kv_repeat)
            v = (v + 0.1 * moe_delta_v.to(v.dtype)).to(v.dtype)

        # ---- Dense attention (no SDPA shortcut when MoE enabled, to keep mask logic uniform) ----
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        if self.is_decoder and not is_cross:
            causal = torch.triu(
                torch.full((L, S), float("-inf"), device=scores.device, dtype=torch.float32),
                diagonal=1 + (S - L),
            )
            scores = scores + causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(1)

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        if eff_mask is not None:
            if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                scores = scores.masked_fill((eff_mask == 0).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 3:
                scores = scores + eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                scores = scores + eff_mask.float()

        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype_in)
        probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        # ---- head_mask (BAM-FIX-10) ----
        if head_mask is not None:
            hm = head_mask.to(probs.dtype)
            if hm.dim() == 1:
                hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 2:
                hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], hm.shape[1], 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 3:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
            probs = probs * hm

        context = torch.matmul(probs.to(v.dtype), v)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        # ---- Output projection ----
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_lin(context)

        if out.shape[-1] != D:
            raise RuntimeError(f"BAMUMoE: output dim {out.shape[-1]} != hidden_size {D}")

        return out, (probs if output_attentions else None)

class BaseAttention(nn.Module):
    """Every custom attention module must inherit from this."""

    def __init__(self, config: Any | None = None, rank: int | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.config = config
        self.rank = rank
        self.num_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            or (
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    getattr(config, "num_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 12)),
                )
                if config is not None
                else 12
            )
        )
        self.hidden_size = (
            kwargs.get("hidden_size")
            or kwargs.get("dim")
            or kwargs.get("d_model")
            or (
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_size",
                    getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "dim", 768)),
                )
                if config is not None
                else 768
            )
        )
        self.head_dim = getattr(
            config,
            "d_kv",
            getattr(
                config,
                "head_dim",
                self.hidden_size // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64,
            ),
        )

    @abstractmethod
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states,
        attention_mask=None,
        head_mask=None,
        output_attentions=False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> tuple[Any, Any | None]:
        """Must return (output, attention_weights_or_None)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("Subclasses of BaseAttention must implement forward()")

    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        """Universal fallback: copy pretrained weights from old attention layer."""
        if old_attn is None:
            return
        import torch

        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        adapter = UniversalAdapter()

        try:
            w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "q")
            w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "k")
            w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "v")
            w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "o")

            with torch.no_grad():
                dim = self.hidden_size
                for q_name in ["W_q", "q_proj", "q_lin", "Wq", "query", "q"]:
                    if hasattr(self, q_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, q_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_q.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_q)
                            if b_q is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_q)

                for k_name in ["W_k", "k_proj", "k_lin", "Wk", "key", "k"]:
                    if hasattr(self, k_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, k_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_k.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_k)
                            if b_k is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_k)

                for v_name in ["W_v", "v_proj", "v_lin", "Wv", "value", "v"]:
                    if hasattr(self, v_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, v_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_v.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_v)
                            if b_v is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_v)

                for o_name in ["out", "out_proj", "o_proj", "out_lin", "proj", "dense"]:
                    if hasattr(self, o_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, o_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_o.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_o)
                            if b_o is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_o)

                # Handle fused in_proj
                if hasattr(self, "in_proj") and hasattr(self.in_proj, "weight"):
                    if self.in_proj.weight.shape == torch.Size([dim * 2, dim]):
                        self.in_proj.weight[:dim].copy_(w_v[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[dim:].copy_(w_q[:dim])
                    elif self.in_proj.weight.shape == torch.Size([dim * 3, dim]):
                        self.in_proj.weight[:dim].copy_(w_q[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[dim : 2 * dim].copy_(w_k[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[2 * dim :].copy_(w_v[:dim])
        except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as e:
            logger.debug("BaseAttention.copy_weights failed: %s", e)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Unexpected error in BaseAttention.copy_weights: %s", e)

    def extra_repr(self) -> str:
        """Extra repr."""
        return f"num_heads={self.num_heads}, head_dim={self.head_dim}, rank={self.rank}"

def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
    """Universal fallback: copy pretrained weights from old attention layer."""
    if old_attn is None:
        return
    import torch

    from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

    adapter = UniversalAdapter()

    try:
        w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "q")
        w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "k")
        w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "v")
        w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "o")

                        mod.weight.copy_(w_q)
                        if b_q is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                            mod.bias.copy_(b_q)

                if hasattr(self, k_name):
                    mod = getattr(self, k_name)

                        mod.weight.copy_(w_k)
                        if b_k is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                            mod.bias.copy_(b_k)

                    mod = getattr(self, v_name)
                    if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_v.shape:
                        mod.weight.copy_(w_v)
                        if b_v is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                            mod.bias.copy_(b_v)

                        mod.weight.copy_(w_o)
                        if b_o is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                            mod.bias.copy_(b_o)

                    self.in_proj.weight[dim : 2 * dim].copy_(w_k[:dim])
                    self.in_proj.weight[2 * dim :].copy_(w_v[:dim])
