@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "cluster", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "cluster_attention",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
class ClusterAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_rope: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        num_clusters: int = 16,
        n_iters: int = 3,
        window_size: int = 32,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        # Resolve hidden_size and num_heads safely even if config is None
        hidden_size = kwargs.get("d_model") or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or kwargs.get("dim")
        num_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_heads") or kwargs.get("n_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
        )
        num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") or kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads")

        if not num_heads and source_attn is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(
                source_attn,
                "num_heads",
                getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    getattr(source_attn, "n_heads", None),
                ),
            )
        if not num_heads and config is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", None))
        num_heads = int(num_heads or 12)

        if not hidden_size and source_attn is not None:
            cand = getattr(
                source_attn,
                "hidden_size",
                getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "embed_dim",
                    getattr(source_attn, "all_head_size", None),
                ),
            )
            if (
                cand is None
                and hasattr(source_attn, "query")
                and hasattr(source_attn.query, "in_features")
            ):
                cand = source_attn.query.in_features
            if (
                cand is None
                and hasattr(source_attn, "q_proj")
                and hasattr(source_attn.q_proj, "in_features")
            ):
                cand = source_attn.q_proj.in_features
            if (
                cand is None
                and hasattr(source_attn, "q_lin")
                and hasattr(source_attn.q_lin, "in_features")
            ):
                cand = source_attn.q_lin.in_features
            if (
                cand is None
                and hasattr(source_attn, "c_attn")
                and hasattr(source_attn.c_attn, "in_features")
            ):
                cand = source_attn.c_attn.in_features
            hidden_size = cand
        if not hidden_size and config is not None:
            hidden_size = getattr(config, "hidden_size", getattr(config, "dim", None))
        hidden_size = int(hidden_size or 768)

        head_dim = kwargs.get("head_dim", 0)
        if not head_dim and source_attn is not None:
            head_dim = getattr(source_attn, "head_dim", 0)
            if not head_dim:
                for attr in ("q_proj", "query", "q_lin"):
                    mod = getattr(source_attn, attr, None)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        w = mod.weight
                        head_dim = w.shape[0] // max(1, num_heads)
                        break

        if not head_dim and config is not None:
            head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", 0)
            if not head_dim:
                try:
                    adapter = ModelAdapter(config)
                    head_dim = getattr(adapter, "head_dim", 0)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        if not head_dim:
            head_dim = hidden_size // max(1, num_heads) if num_heads > 0 else 64
        head_dim = int(head_dim)

        if not num_kv_heads and source_attn is not None:
            for attr in ("k_proj", "key", "k_lin"):
                mod = getattr(source_attn, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    w = mod.weight
                    num_kv_heads = w.shape[0] // max(1, head_dim)
                    break
            if not num_kv_heads:
                num_kv_heads = getattr(source_attn, "num_key_value_heads", None) or getattr(
                    source_attn, "num_kv_heads", None
                )

        if not num_kv_heads and config is not None:
            num_kv_heads = getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", None)

        num_kv_heads = int(num_kv_heads or num_heads)

        self.hidden_size: int = hidden_size
        self.d_model: int = hidden_size
        self.num_heads: int = num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads: int = num_kv_heads
        self.head_dim: int = head_dim
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat: int = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        if config is not None:
            self.num_clusters = getattr(config, "cluster_num_clusters", num_clusters)
        else:
            self.num_clusters = num_clusters
        # Fast K-means iterations (default 3, removed max(20, n_iters) constraint)
        self.n_iters: int = max(1, n_iters)
        self.window_size: int = window_size
        self.temp = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.1))
        self.local_residual = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.5))

        # Pre-allocated index buffer for efficient windowed attention
        self.register_buffer("_seq_idx", torch.arange(4096), persistent=False)

        # Decoder & nested encoder detection with robust prefix matching
        model_type = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if config else ""
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        is_dec = (
            (
                getattr(config, "is_decoder", False)
                or getattr(config, "is_causal", False)
                or getattr(config, "causal", False)
                or any(model_type.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            )
            if config
            else False
        )
        if hasattr(config, "architectures") and any(
            "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
            for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if source_attn is not None:
            cls_name = source_attn.__class__.__name__.lower()
            if (
                any(p in cls_name for p in ("gpt", "causal", "qwen", "llama", "mistral"))
                or getattr(source_attn, "is_causal", False)
                or getattr(source_attn, "is_decoder", False)
                or hasattr(source_attn, "c_attn")
            ):
                is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder") is not None:
            is_dec = bool(kwargs["is_decoder"])
        elif kwargs.get("is_causal") is not None:
            is_dec = bool(kwargs["is_causal"])
        self.is_decoder: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = bool(kwargs.get("is_nested_encoder", False))

        if config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)),
            )
            self.lora_alpha = getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
        elif comp_config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank))
            self.lora_alpha = getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
        else:
            self.lora_rank = kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)
            self.lora_alpha = kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)
        self.freeze_base: bool = self.lora_rank > 0

        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_dropout_prob",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
                ),
            )
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)
        self.rotary_emb = (
            getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn is not None else None
        )

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn, **kwargs)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. HuggingFace / standard UniversalAdapter extraction
        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"UniversalAdapter extraction failed for {proj}: {e}")

        # 2. GPT-2 Fused c_attn Conv1D handling (including LoRALinear wrappers)
        if (
            hasattr(src, "c_attn")
            and src.c_attn is not None
            and (
                extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
            )
        ):
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            if hasattr(c_attn_mod, "merge") and not getattr(c_attn_mod, "merged", False):
                try:
                    c_attn_mod.merge()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if weight is not None:
                weight = weight.data.clone()
                p_cls = getattr(base_c, "__class__", type(base_c)).__name__
                if (
                    ("Conv1D" in p_cls)
                    or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or ("Conv1D" in getattr(c_attn_mod, "__class__", type(c_attn_mod)).__name__)
                    or hasattr(c_attn_mod, "nf")
                    or (weight.ndim == 2 and weight.shape[1] == 3 * self.hidden_size)
                ):
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                bias_data = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    weight[:split_size, :],
                    bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    (bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None),
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    weight[2 * split_size :, :],
                    bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            if hasattr(c_proj_mod, "merge") and not getattr(c_proj_mod, "merged", False):
                try:
                    c_proj_mod.merge()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                p_cls = getattr(base_o, "__class__", type(base_o)).__name__
                if (
                    ("Conv1D" in p_cls)
                    or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                    or ("Conv1D" in getattr(c_proj_mod, "__class__", type(c_proj_mod)).__name__)
                    or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                    or (w_o.ndim == 2 and w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1])
                ):
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (
                    w_o,
                    bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. RoBERTa / BERT Nested Encoder handling
        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query"):
                extracted["q"] = (s.query.weight.data, getattr(s.query, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "key"):
                extracted["k"] = (s.key.weight.data, getattr(s.key, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "value"):
                extracted["v"] = (s.value.weight.data, getattr(s.value, "bias", None))

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            dense = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (dense.weight.data, getattr(dense, "bias", None))

        # 4. Modern Decoders (Qwen3.5, LLaMA, Mistral) & DistilBERT
        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        if hasattr(layer, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                layer.weight.data,
                                getattr(layer, "bias", None),
                            )
                            break

        # Detect nested encoder architecture
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, attr)
            for attr in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
                or (
                    ("roberta" in cls_name.lower() or "bert" in cls_name.lower())
                    and not has_out_proj
                )
            )
        ):
            is_nested = True
        if hasattr(src, "config"):
            if (
                getattr(src.config, "model_type", "").lower() in ("roberta", "bert", "xlm-roberta")
                and not has_out_proj
            ):
                is_nested = True
        if kwargs.get("is_nested_encoder") is not None:
            is_nested = kwargs["is_nested_encoder"]
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        # Check required weights
        missing = [k for k in ["q", "k", "v"] if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"ClusterAttention missing required weights for projections: {missing}"
            )

        # Frobenius norm validation
        for proj_key in ["q", "k", "v"]:
            w = extracted[proj_key][0]
            if w is not None:
                norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float())
                if norm < 1e-4:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        f"ClusterAttention extracted weight for {proj_key} has near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm.item():.6f})"
                    )

        if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
            norm_o = torch.linalg.norm(extracted["o"][0].float())
            if norm_o < 1e-4:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"ClusterAttention extracted weight for output projection has near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm_o.item():.6f})"
                )

        # Copy into projection modules
        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in [
                ("q", self.q_proj),
                ("k", self.k_proj),
                ("v", self.v_proj),
                ("o", self.out_proj),
            ]:
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    target_w = target.weight
                    out_d, in_d = target_w.shape
                    src_out, src_in = w.shape
                    target_w.data[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)].copy_(
                        w[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)]
                    )
                    if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                        b_val = b.data if isinstance(b, nn.Parameter) else b
                        target.bias.data[: min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                            b_val[: min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))]
                        )
                elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    min_d = min(target.weight.shape[0], target.weight.shape[1])
                    target.weight.data.zero_()
                    target.weight.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    if target.bias is not None:
                        target.bias.data.zero_()

                target.freeze_base_weights()

        if hasattr(src, "rotary_emb") and src.rotary_emb is not None:
            self.rotary_emb = src.rotary_emb

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    def _kmeans_cluster(
        self,
        Q: torch.Tensor,
        K_num: int,
        iters: int = 3,
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        r"""
        Vectorized Lloyd's $K$-means clustering for queries with padding token filtering.
        Q: [B, H, L, d], valid_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] of shape [B, L] (True for real tokens)
        -> centroids: [B, H, K, d], cluster_ids: [B, H, L], counts: [B, H, K, 1]
        """
        B, H, L, d = Q.shape
        K = min(K_num, L)

        if valid_mask is not None:
            valid_f = valid_mask.float()  # [B, L]
            num_valid = valid_f.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1.0)  # [B, 1]
            step = (num_valid / K).clamp(min=1.0)  # [B, 1]
            k_idx = torch.arange(K, device=Q.device, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0)  # [1, K]
            target_ranks = (k_idx * step).clamp(max=num_valid - 1.0).long()  # [B, K]

            # Assign rank to each valid token: 0, 1, ..., num_valid-1
            ranks = (valid_f.cumsum(dim=-1) - 1.0) * valid_f  # [B, L]
            is_valid_3d = valid_mask.unsqueeze(1)  # [B, 1, L]
            rank_diff = torch.where(
                is_valid_3d,
                (ranks.unsqueeze(1) - target_ranks.unsqueeze(-1)).abs(),
                torch.tensor(1e6, device=Q.device),
            )
            chosen_indices = torch.argmin(rank_diff, dim=-1)  # [B, K]

            idx_expanded = chosen_indices.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).expand(B, H, K, d)
            centroids = torch.gather(Q, 2, idx_expanded).clone()
        else:
            step = max(1, L // K)
            init_indices = torch.arange(0, L, step, device=Q.device)[:K]
            if len(init_indices) < K:
                pad_idx = torch.zeros(K - len(init_indices), dtype=torch.long, device=Q.device)
                init_indices = torch.cat([init_indices, pad_idx])
            centroids = Q[:, :, init_indices, :].clone()

        cluster_ids = torch.zeros(B, H, L, dtype=torch.long, device=Q.device)
        counts = torch.ones(B, H, K, 1, dtype=Q.dtype, device=Q.device)
        Q_f = Q.float()

        for _ in range(iters):
            centroids_f = centroids.float()
            dist = torch.cdist(Q_f, centroids_f)
            if valid_mask is not None:
                # Disincentivize padding tokens from pulling centroids
                dist = dist.masked_fill(~valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1), 1e6)
            cluster_ids = torch.argmin(dist, dim=-1)

            one_hot = F.one_hot(cluster_ids, num_classes=K).to(dtype=Q.dtype)
            if valid_mask is not None:
                one_hot = one_hot * valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype=Q.dtype)

            counts = one_hot.sum(dim=2).unsqueeze(-1)
            counts_clamped = counts.clamp(min=1.0)
            sum_q = torch.einsum("bhlk,bhld->bhkd", one_hot, Q)
            centroids = torch.where(counts > 0, sum_q / (counts_clamped + 1e-8), centroids)
            centroids = torch.nan_to_num(centroids, nan=0.0)

        return centroids, cluster_ids, counts

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        key_value_states = kwargs.get("key_value_states", encoder_hidden_states)
        is_cross_attention = key_value_states is not None
        kv_input = key_value_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states

        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        N_k = kv_input.shape[1]
        H, Dh = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # Apply RoPE (accept from position_embeddings or fallback to rotary_emb)
        pos_emb = (
            position_embeddings
            if position_embeddings is not None
            else kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        )
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) >= 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            rotary_mod = self.rotary_emb
            pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
            if pos_ids is None:
                pos_ids = torch.arange(L, device=device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
            try:
                cos, sin = rotary_mod(Q, seq_len=L)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    cos, sin = rotary_mod(Q, pos_ids)
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        cos, sin = rotary_mod(Q)
                    except Exception:
                        cos, sin = None, None
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        scale = Dh**-0.5

        # Check for causal decoding (guard against self.config being None)
        _cfg = self.config
        is_dec_cfg = False
        if _cfg is not None:
            model_type = str(getattr(_cfg, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            _decoder_prefixes = (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
            is_dec_cfg = (
                getattr(_cfg, "is_decoder", False)
                or getattr(_cfg, "is_causal", False)
                or getattr(_cfg, "causal", False)
                or any(model_type.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            )
        is_decoder = self.is_decoder or is_dec_cfg

        if is_decoder or is_cross_attention:
            # Exact attention with causal masking to prevent future token leakage
            scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")
            if mask is not None:
                if mask.dim() == 4:
                    if mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                        scores = scores.masked_fill(~mask, -10000.0)
                    else:
                        scores = scores + mask.to(scores.dtype)
                else:
                    valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N_k, device)
                    if valid_m is not None:
                        scores = scores.masked_fill((~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0)

            if is_decoder:
                causal_tril = (
                    torch.tril(torch.ones((L, N_k), device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                )
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal_tril, -10000.0)

            attn = torch.nan_to_num(F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0).to(dtype)
            attn = self.dropout(attn)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
                attn = attn * head_mask

            out_context = torch.matmul(attn, V)
            if not is_decoder:
                valid_q = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device)
                if valid_q is not None:
                    out_context = out_context * valid_q.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype)
            attn_ret = attn if output_attentions else None
        else:
            # Bidirectional clustering attention
            valid_mask_2d = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device)
            key_valid_4d = (
                valid_mask_2d.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                if valid_mask_2d is not None
                else torch.ones((B, 1, 1, L), dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
            )

            if L <= 64 and (self.num_clusters >= 8 or not output_attentions):
                scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~key_valid_4d, -10000.0)
                attn = torch.nan_to_num(F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0).to(dtype)
                attn = self.dropout(attn)
                out_context = torch.matmul(attn, V)
                attn_ret = attn if output_attentions else None
            else:
                K_num = min(max(1, self.num_clusters), L)

                # 1. Cluster Queries into K centroids with padding mask filtering
                centroids, cluster_ids, counts = self._kmeans_cluster(
                    Q, K_num, self.n_iters, valid_mask=valid_mask_2d
                )

                # 2. Centroid-to-Key Global Cross-Attention [B, H, K, L]
                scores_cent = torch.matmul(centroids, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                scores_cent = scores_cent.masked_fill(~key_valid_4d, -10000.0)
                attn_cent = torch.nan_to_num(F.softmax(scores_cent.float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0).to(
                    dtype
                )
                attn_cent = self.dropout(attn_cent)

                # Centroid values: [B, H, K, Dh]
                V_cent = torch.matmul(attn_cent, V)

                # 3. Differentiable Query-to-Centroid soft assignment with learned sharpening & context gathering [B, H, L, Dh]
                scores_assign = torch.matmul(Q, centroids.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                empty_clusters = (counts == 0).squeeze(-1).unsqueeze(2)  # [B, H, 1, K]
                scores_assign = scores_assign.masked_fill(empty_clusters, -10000.0)
                temp_clamped = self.temp.abs().clamp(min=0.1, max=10.0)
                attn_assign = torch.nan_to_num(
                    F.softmax((scores_assign / temp_clamped).float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0
                ).to(dtype)
                assign_denom = attn_assign.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1.0)
                attn_assign = attn_assign / assign_denom
                global_context = torch.matmul(attn_assign, V_cent)

                # 4. Optional Local Sliding Window Attention
                if self.window_size > 0 and self.window_size < L:
                    scores_loc = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                    idx = (
                        self._seq_idx[:L].to(device)
                        if hasattr(self, "_seq_idx") and len(self._seq_idx) >= L
                        else torch.arange(L, device=device)
                    )
                    # FIXED: Failure Mode 1 (Mode Collapse): Causal band mask prevents future token leakage
                    if is_decoder:
                        band_mask = (idx.unsqueeze(1) >= idx.unsqueeze(0)) & ((idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)) <= self.window_size)
                    else:
                        band_mask = (idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)).abs() <= self.window_size
                    band_mask_4d = band_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0) & key_valid_4d
                    scores_loc = scores_loc.masked_fill(~band_mask_4d, -10000.0)
                    attn_loc = torch.nan_to_num(F.softmax(scores_loc.float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0).to(
                        dtype
                    )
                    attn_loc = self.dropout(attn_loc)
                    local_context = torch.matmul(attn_loc, V)
                    alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.local_residual)
                    out_context = (1.0 - alpha) * global_context + alpha * local_context
                else:
                    out_context = global_context

                attn_ret = attn_cent if output_attentions else None

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
                out_context = out_context * head_mask

            if valid_mask_2d is not None:
                out_context = out_context * valid_mask_2d.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(dtype)

        out = out_context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = out
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(out)

        valid_out = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device)
        if valid_out is not None:
            output = output * valid_out.unsqueeze(-1).to(output.dtype)

        return output, attn_ret

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

class ExpertChoiceMoE (d_model: int,

Expand source code 
class ExpertChoiceMoE(nn.Module):
    r"""
    Expert Choice Routing MoE layer.
    Each expert selects top-k tokens with the highest affinity scores.
    Equipped with zero-initialized final layers for step-0 representation preservation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        d_model: int,
        n_experts: int = 2,
        capacity_factor: float = 1.0,
        noise_std: float = 0.05,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.capacity_factor = capacity_factor
        self.noise_std = noise_std
        self.aux_loss: torch.Tensor = torch.tensor(0.0)

        self.router_ln = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model)
        self.router = nn.Linear(d_model, n_experts, bias=False)
        nn.init.uniform_(self.router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)

        expert_hidden_dim = max(32, d_model // 4)
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList(
            [
                nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(d_model, expert_hidden_dim),
                    nn.GELU(),
                    nn.Linear(expert_hidden_dim, d_model),
                )
                for _ in range(n_experts)
            ]
        )

        # Active MoE expert representation
        for expert in self.experts:
            nn.init.normal_(expert[2].weight, std=0.02)
            if expert[2].bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(expert[2].bias)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        r"""Forward execution using Expert Choice routing."""
        B, L, D = x.shape
        x_flat = x.view(-1, D)
        T = x_flat.size(0)

        if self.d_model == D:
            logits = self.router(self.router_ln(x_flat))
        else:
            normed = F.layer_norm(x_flat, (D,))
            if not hasattr(self, "_dynamic_router") or self._dynamic_router.in_features != D:
                self._dynamic_router = nn.Linear(D, self.n_experts, bias=False).to(
                    device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype
                )
                nn.init.uniform_(self._dynamic_router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)
            logits = self._dynamic_router(normed)

        if self.training and self.noise_std > 0:
            logits = logits + torch.randn_like(logits) * self.noise_std

        # FIXED: Converted to Causal Token-Choice MoE routing to prevent future token leakage.
        # Expert-Choice applies softmax over the sequence (dim=0), which catastrophically leaks future tokens into past states.
        # We now apply softmax over the experts (dim=-1) and select top-k experts per token.
        gate_scores = F.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1).to(dtype=logits.dtype)
        
        # Select top-2 experts per token (standard MoE)
        top_k_experts = min(2, self.n_experts)
        topk_vals, topk_indices = torch.topk(gate_scores, top_k_experts, dim=-1)

        out_flat = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
        for e in range(self.n_experts):
            # Find tokens assigned to expert e
            expert_mask = topk_indices == e
            token_indices = expert_mask.any(dim=-1).nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
            
            if token_indices.numel() == 0:
                continue

            # Get the gating values for the selected tokens
            # expert_mask is [T, top_k], so we need to extract the matching topk_vals
            matched_vals = topk_vals[expert_mask]

            selected_x = x_flat[token_indices]
            
            if selected_x.size(-1) == self.d_model:
                expert_out = self.experts[e](selected_x)
            else:
                if (
                    not hasattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
                    or getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}").in_features != D
                ):
                    setattr(
                        self,
                        f"_expert_in_proj_{e}",
                        nn.Linear(D, self.d_model).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                    )
                    setattr(
                        self,
                        f"_expert_out_proj_{e}",
                        nn.Linear(self.d_model, D).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                    )
                proj_in = getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
                proj_out = getattr(self, f"_expert_out_proj_{e}")
                expert_out = proj_out(self.experts[e](proj_in(selected_x)))

            update_val = (
                expert_out.to(out_flat.dtype) * matched_vals.unsqueeze(-1).to(out_flat.dtype)
            ).to(dtype=out_flat.dtype)
            
            out_flat.index_add_(0, token_indices, update_val)

        # Auxiliary load balancing loss
        prob_mean = F.softmax(logits.float() / 1.0, dim=-1).mean(dim=0)
        self.aux_loss = self.n_experts * torch.sum(prob_mean**2)

        return out_flat.view(B, L, D)

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    r"""Forward execution using Expert Choice routing."""
    B, L, D = x.shape
    x_flat = x.view(-1, D)
    T = x_flat.size(0)

    if self.d_model == D:
        logits = self.router(self.router_ln(x_flat))
    else:
        normed = F.layer_norm(x_flat, (D,))
        if not hasattr(self, "_dynamic_router") or self._dynamic_router.in_features != D:
            self._dynamic_router = nn.Linear(D, self.n_experts, bias=False).to(
                device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype
            )
            nn.init.uniform_(self._dynamic_router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)
        logits = self._dynamic_router(normed)

    if self.training and self.noise_std > 0:
        logits = logits + torch.randn_like(logits) * self.noise_std

    # FIXED: Converted to Causal Token-Choice MoE routing to prevent future token leakage.
    # Expert-Choice applies softmax over the sequence (dim=0), which catastrophically leaks future tokens into past states.
    # We now apply softmax over the experts (dim=-1) and select top-k experts per token.
    gate_scores = F.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1).to(dtype=logits.dtype)
    
    # Select top-2 experts per token (standard MoE)
    top_k_experts = min(2, self.n_experts)
    topk_vals, topk_indices = torch.topk(gate_scores, top_k_experts, dim=-1)

    out_flat = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
    for e in range(self.n_experts):
        # Find tokens assigned to expert e
        expert_mask = topk_indices == e
        token_indices = expert_mask.any(dim=-1).nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
        
        if token_indices.numel() == 0:
            continue

        # Get the gating values for the selected tokens
        # expert_mask is [T, top_k], so we need to extract the matching topk_vals
        matched_vals = topk_vals[expert_mask]

        selected_x = x_flat[token_indices]
        
        if selected_x.size(-1) == self.d_model:
            expert_out = self.experts[e](selected_x)
        else:
            if (
                not hasattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
                or getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}").in_features != D
            ):
                setattr(
                    self,
                    f"_expert_in_proj_{e}",
                    nn.Linear(D, self.d_model).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                )
                setattr(
                    self,
                    f"_expert_out_proj_{e}",
                    nn.Linear(self.d_model, D).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                )
            proj_in = getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
            proj_out = getattr(self, f"_expert_out_proj_{e}")
            expert_out = proj_out(self.experts[e](proj_in(selected_x)))

        update_val = (
            expert_out.to(out_flat.dtype) * matched_vals.unsqueeze(-1).to(out_flat.dtype)
        ).to(dtype=out_flat.dtype)
        
        out_flat.index_add_(0, token_indices, update_val)

    # Auxiliary load balancing loss
    prob_mean = F.softmax(logits.float() / 1.0, dim=-1).mean(dim=0)
    self.aux_loss = self.n_experts * torch.sum(prob_mean**2)

    return out_flat.view(B, L, D)

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "expert_choice_moe",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "expert_choice_moe_attention",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
class ExpertChoiceMoEAttention(BaseAttention):
    r"""Expert Choice MoE Multi-Head Attention Module."""

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        n_experts: int = 2,
        capacity_factor: float = 1.0,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        layer_id: int | None = None,
        layer_idx: int | None = None,
        moe_init_gate: float = 0.0,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn

        cfg_obj = getattr(config, "text_config", config) if config is not None else None
        hidden_size = getattr(cfg_obj, "hidden_size", None) if cfg_obj is not None else None
        num_heads = getattr(cfg_obj, "num_attention_heads", None) if cfg_obj is not None else None

        if hidden_size is None and source_attn is not None:
            for cand in ["q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "c_attn", "in_proj_weight", "qkv"]:
                mod = getattr(source_attn, cand, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    w = mod.weight
                    hidden_size = w.shape[1] if w.ndim == 2 else None
                    break
                elif isinstance(mod, torch.Tensor) and mod.ndim == 2:
                    hidden_size = mod.shape[1]
                    break
        if hidden_size is None:
            hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
        if num_heads is None:
            num_heads = kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)

        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = int(hidden_size)
        self.num_heads = int(num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = (
            getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads)
            if config is not None
            else self.num_heads
        )
        self.head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) or (self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads))
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        # Static layer index
        resolved_layer_id = (
            layer_id
            if layer_id is not None
            else (
                layer_idx
                if layer_idx is not None
                else kwargs.get("layer_id", kwargs.get("layer_idx", 0))
            )
        )
        self.layer_idx: int = int(resolved_layer_id)

        # LoRA adaptation configuration
        self.lora_rank: int = getattr(
            config,
            "lora_rank",
            getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
        )
        self.lora_alpha: float = getattr(
            config,
            "lora_alpha",
            getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
        )
        self.freeze_base: bool = self.lora_rank > 0

        # Decoder & nested encoder detection
        model_type = getattr(config, "model_type", "").lower()
        is_dec = getattr(
            config,
            "is_decoder",
            getattr(config, "is_causal", kwargs.get("is_causal", False)),
        )
        decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if (
            any(model_type.startswith(p) for p in decoder_prefixes)
            or hasattr(config, "architectures")
            and any(
                "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        # Multi-head projections equipped with LoRA adapters
        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        self.moe = ExpertChoiceMoE(
            self.all_head_size, n_experts=n_experts, capacity_factor=capacity_factor
        )
        # Trainable gate for MoE output contribution (initialized with negative bias for smooth ramp-up)
        self.moe_gate = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(float(moe_init_gate)))

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)
        self.aux_loss: torch.Tensor = torch.tensor(0.0)

        self.rotary_emb = None
        self.comp_config: Any | None = comp_config

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # Compatibility properties
    @property
    def query(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def key(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def value(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.v_lin

    @property
    def dense(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def o_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def out_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def q_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def k_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def v_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.v_lin

    @property
    def W_q(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def W_k(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def W_v(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.v_lin

    @property
    def c_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.out_lin

    def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
        r"""Copy projection weights from pretrained attention module with Frobenius norm verification."""
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            return

        cls_name = pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = (
            hasattr(pretrained_attn, "out_lin")
            or hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_proj")
            or hasattr(pretrained_attn, "o_proj")
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb"):
            self.rotary_emb = pretrained_attn.rotary_emb

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2: Fused c_attn + c_proj
        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn") and pretrained_attn.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = pretrained_attn.c_attn
            if hasattr(c_attn_mod, "merge") and not getattr(c_attn_mod, "merged", False):
                try:
                    c_attn_mod.merge()
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
                    logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_proj") and pretrained_attn.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = pretrained_attn.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa / BERT nested self-attention
        elif hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query"):
            s = pretrained_attn.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = pretrained_attn.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Standard linear projections (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query", "W_q"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key", "W_k"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value", "W_v"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(pretrained_attn, name):
                        mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # Fallback via UniversalAdapter
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(pretrained_attn, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                f"ExpertChoiceMoEAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} "
                f"from module {pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__}. Proceeding with partially initialized projections."
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_lin,
            "k": self.k_lin,
            "v": self.v_lin,
            "o": self.out_lin,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

                if (
                    w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                    and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape
                ):
                    w = w.t().contiguous()
                out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))

                target_mod.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(
                        device=target_mod.weight.device, dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype
                    )
                )

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    min_b = min(target_mod.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target_mod.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(
                        b[:min_b].to(device=target_mod.bias.device, dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype)
                    )
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                # Frobenius norm verification (>= 1e-4)
                w_norm = float(torch.norm(target_mod.weight.data).item())
                if w_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"ExpertChoiceMoEAttention weight copy verification failed for '{proj_key}': norm too small ({w_norm:.2e})"
                    )

                if self.freeze_base:
                    target_mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape

        is_cross_attention = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states
        kv_len = kv_input.shape[1]

        # 1. Projections
        q = (
            self.q_lin(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        k = (
            self.k_lin(kv_input)
            .view(B, kv_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.v_lin(kv_input)
            .view(B, kv_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # 2. Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE)
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross_attention:
            if isinstance(position_embeddings, tuple):
                cos, sin = position_embeddings
            else:
                cos, sin = position_embeddings, None
            if sin is not None:
                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids)
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None and not is_cross_attention:
            if position_ids is not None:
                cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(v, seq_len=kv_len)
                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids)

        # 3. Grouped Query Attention (GQA) head expansion
        if self.num_kv_heads != self.num_heads:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        # 4. Multi-head attention scores
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        # Memory-efficient: use SDPA to avoid OOM on large seqs

        try:
            _qs = q.half() if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
            _ks = k.half() if k.dtype == torch.float32 else k
            _vs = v.half() if v.dtype == torch.float32 else v
            _is_causal = getattr(self, "is_decoder", False) and not is_cross_attention and L > 1
            _sdpa_out = torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                _qs,
                _ks,
                _vs,
                is_causal=_is_causal,
                dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
            )
            attn_out = (
                _sdpa_out.to(hidden_states.dtype)
                .transpose(1, 2)
                .contiguous()
                .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
            )
            attn_out = torch.nan_to_num(attn_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            moe_out = self.moe(attn_out)
            self.aux_loss = self.moe.aux_loss
            moe_weight = torch.sigmoid(self.moe_gate).to(attn_out.dtype)
            enriched_attn = (attn_out + moe_weight * moe_out.to(attn_out.dtype)).to(attn_out.dtype)
            if self.is_nested_encoder and enriched_attn.shape[-1] == self.dim:
                out = enriched_attn
            else:
                out = self.out_lin(enriched_attn)
            if out.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
                out = (
                    F.pad(out, (0, hidden_states.shape[-1] - out.shape[-1]))
                    if out.shape[-1] < hidden_states.shape[-1]
                    else out[..., : hidden_states.shape[-1]]
                )
            return out, None
        except Exception:
            pass

        scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        if self.is_decoder and not is_cross_attention:
            causal_mask = torch.triu(
                torch.full(
                    (L, kv_len),
                    float("-inf"),
                    device=scores.device,
                    dtype=torch.float32,
                ),
                diagonal=1,
            )
            scores = scores + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross_attention else attention_mask
        if eff_mask is not None:
            if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                pad_mask = (eff_mask == 0).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                scores = scores.masked_fill(pad_mask, float("-inf"))
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 3:
                scores = scores + eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                scores = scores + eff_mask.float()

        probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        # 5. Head mask handling
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.shape[0], head_mask.shape[1], 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            probs = probs * head_mask

        attn_out = (
            torch.matmul(probs.to(v.dtype), v)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
            .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        )

        # 6. MoE feedforward enrichment with gate blending
        moe_out = self.moe(attn_out)
        self.aux_loss = self.moe.aux_loss
        moe_weight = torch.sigmoid(self.moe_gate).to(attn_out.dtype)
        enriched_attn = (attn_out + moe_weight * moe_out.to(attn_out.dtype)).to(attn_out.dtype)

        # 7. Output projection with nested encoder bypass
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            if enriched_attn.shape[-1] != self.dim:
                out = self.out_lin(enriched_attn)
            else:
                out = enriched_attn
        else:
            out = self.out_lin(enriched_attn)

        if out.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
            out = (
                F.pad(out, (0, hidden_states.shape[-1] - out.shape[-1]))
                if out.shape[-1] < hidden_states.shape[-1]
                else out[..., : hidden_states.shape[-1]]
            )

        return out, (probs if output_attentions else None)

Expert Choice MoE Multi-Head Attention Module.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

        return

            "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query", "W_q"],

            "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value", "W_v"],

                if w is not None:
                    extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

    missing_after = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing_after:
        logger.warning(
            f"ExpertChoiceMoEAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} "
            f"from module {pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__}. Proceeding with partially initialized projections."
        )

    target_map = {
        "q": self.q_lin,
        "k": self.k_lin,
        "v": self.v_lin,
        "o": self.out_lin,
    }

    with torch.no_grad():
        for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is None:
                if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                    if target_mod.bias is not None:
                        nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                continue

            if w.ndim != 2:
                w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

            if (
                w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape
            ):
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
            in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))

            target_mod.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                w[:out_d, :in_d].to(
                    device=target_mod.weight.device, dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype
                )
            )

            if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                min_b = min(target_mod.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                target_mod.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(
                    b[:min_b].to(device=target_mod.bias.device, dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype)
                )
            elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

            # Frobenius norm verification (>= 1e-4)
            w_norm = float(torch.norm(target_mod.weight.data).item())
            if w_norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"ExpertChoiceMoEAttention weight copy verification failed for '{proj_key}': norm too small ({w_norm:.2e})"
                )

            if self.freeze_base:
                target_mod.freeze_base_weights()

Copy projection weights from pretrained attention module with Frobenius norm verification.

Expand source code 
class FactorisedLoRALinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Low-rank factorized linear with optional LoRA:

        y = (x V S U^T) + (x lora_A^T lora_B^T) * scaling + b

    Base factors (U, S, V) are frozen parameters (visible in .parameters()).
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        svd_rank: int,
        lora_rank: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        bias: bool = True,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = int(in_features)
        self.out_features = int(out_features)
        self.svd_rank = max(1, min(svd_rank, self.in_features, self.out_features))
        self.lora_rank = max(0, int(lora_rank))
        self.lora_scaling = (lora_alpha / self.lora_rank) if self.lora_rank > 0 else 1.0

        fk = {"dtype": dtype, "device": device}
        self.U = nn.Parameter(
            torch.zeros(out_features, self.svd_rank, **fk),
            requires_grad=False,
        )
        self.S = nn.Parameter(
            torch.ones(self.svd_rank, **fk),
            requires_grad=False,
        )
        self.V = nn.Parameter(
            torch.zeros(in_features, self.svd_rank, **fk),
            requires_grad=False,
        )

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_features, **fk), requires_grad=False)
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, in_features, **fk))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_features, self.lora_rank, **fk))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

    def set_svd_factors(self, W: torch.Tensor, bias: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W_f = W.float()
            if W_f.shape != (self.out_features, self.in_features):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"FactorisedLoRALinear: shape mismatch "
                    f"{tuple(W_f.shape)} vs ({self.out_features}, {self.in_features})"
                )
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_f, full_matrices=False)
            r = min(self.svd_rank, S.shape[0])
            self.U.data.zero_()
            self.S.data.zero_()
            self.V.data.zero_()
            self.U.data[:, :r].copy_(U[:, :r].to(device=self.U.device, dtype=self.U.dtype))
            self.S.data[:r].copy_(S[:r].to(device=self.S.device, dtype=self.S.dtype))
            self.V.data[:, :r].copy_(
                Vt[:r, :].t().contiguous().to(device=self.V.device, dtype=self.V.dtype)
            )
            if bias is not None and self.bias is not None:
                n = min(self.bias.numel(), bias.numel())
                self.bias.data[:n].copy_(
                    bias[:n].to(device=self.bias.device, dtype=self.bias.dtype)
                )

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        orig_dtype = x.dtype
        x_f = x.float()
        xV = torch.matmul(x_f, self.V.float())
        xV_S = xV * self.S.float().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
        out = torch.matmul(xV_S, self.U.float().t().contiguous())
        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias.float()
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            xa = torch.matmul(x_f, self.lora_A.float().t().contiguous())
            out = out + torch.matmul(xa, self.lora_B.float().t().contiguous()) * self.lora_scaling
        return out.to(orig_dtype)

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        W = (self.U.float() * self.S.float().unsqueeze(0)) @ self.V.float().t().contiguous()
        return W

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        self.U.requires_grad = False
        self.S.requires_grad = False
        self.V.requires_grad = False
        if self.bias is not None:
            self.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_A is not None:
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_B is not None:
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

Low-rank factorized linear with optional LoRA:

y = (x V S U^T) + (x lora_A^T lora_B^T) * scaling + b

Base factors (U, S, V) are frozen parameters (visible in .parameters()).

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

        self.V.data[:, :r].copy_(

Expand source code 
class FactorizedLoRALinear(nn.Module):
    """
    SVD-factorized linear + LoRA adapter.
    Forward: y = up(down(x)) + (lora_B @ lora_A)(x) * scaling
    Base (down, up) frozen; LoRA trainable.
    """

    is_custom_attention = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int = 64,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        bias: bool = True,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = int(in_features)
        self.out_features = int(out_features)
        self.rank = max(1, min(int(rank), self.in_features, self.out_features))
        self.svd_rank = self.rank
        self.lora_rank = int(lora_rank) if lora_rank > 0 else 0
        self.lora_scaling = (lora_alpha / lora_rank) if lora_rank > 0 else 1.0

        factory = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.down = nn.Linear(self.in_features, self.rank, bias=False, **factory)
        self.up = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.out_features, bias=bias, **factory)

        self.down.weight.requires_grad = False
        self.up.weight.requires_grad = False
        if self.up.bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.requires_grad = False

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.in_features, **factory))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.out_features, self.lora_rank, **factory))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        w = torch.matmul(self.up.weight, self.down.weight)
        if self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            w = w + (self.lora_B @ self.lora_A) * self.lora_scaling
        return w

    @property
    def bias(self) -> torch.Tensor | None:
        return self.up.bias

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        self.down.weight.requires_grad = False
        self.up.weight.requires_grad = False
        if self.up.bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_A is not None:
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_B is not None:
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        out = self.up(self.down(x))
        if self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            out = out + F.linear(F.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B) * self.lora_scaling
        return out
