Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("hamming_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class HammingAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_rope: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        distill_temperature: float = 2.0,
        sparsity_ratio: float = 0.0,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        num_heads, num_kv_heads, hidden_size, head_dim, kv_repeat = 0, 0, 0, 0, 1

        # HAD-FIX-07: ModelAdapter failures are surfaced (debug), not silently swallowed
        if config is not None and _HAS_MODEL_ADAPTER:
            try:
                self.adapter = ModelAdapter(config)
                num_heads = self.adapter.num_heads
                num_kv_heads = self.adapter.num_key_value_heads
                hidden_size = self.adapter.hidden_size
                head_dim = self.adapter.head_dim
                kv_repeat = self.adapter.kv_repeat
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("ModelAdapter failed: %s", e)

        # Fallback to direct config attributes
        if not hidden_size and config is not None:
            hidden_size = getattr(
                config,
                "hidden_size",
                getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "dim", 0)),
            )
        if not num_heads and config is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                getattr(config, "num_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 0)),
            )

        # Fallback to source attention module
        if not hidden_size and pretrained_attn is not None:
            hidden_size = getattr(
                pretrained_attn, "hidden_size", getattr(pretrained_attn, "embed_dim", 0)
            )
            if not hidden_size:
                for cand in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "c_attn"):
                    m = getattr(pretrained_attn, cand, None)
                    if m is not None and hasattr(m, "in_features"):
                        hidden_size = m.in_features
                        break
                if not hidden_size:
                    out_l = getattr(
                        pretrained_attn,
                        "out_lin",
                        getattr(
                            pretrained_attn,
                            "dense",
                            getattr(pretrained_attn, "c_proj", None),
                        ),
                    )
                    if out_l is not None and hasattr(out_l, "out_features"):
                        hidden_size = out_l.out_features

        # Final defaults
        hidden_size = hidden_size or kwargs.get("hidden_size", kwargs.get("d_model", 768))
        num_heads = num_heads or kwargs.get("num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12))
        num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads or _cfg_get(
            config, "num_key_value_heads", kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", num_heads)
        )
        head_dim = head_dim or kwargs.get("head_dim") or (hidden_size // max(1, num_heads))
        kv_repeat = kv_repeat or max(1, num_heads // max(1, num_kv_heads))

        self.num_heads: int = int(num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads: int = int(num_kv_heads)
        self.hidden_size: int = int(hidden_size)
        self.head_dim: int = int(head_dim)
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat: int = int(kv_repeat)

        # HAD-FIX-06: keep API compat args but they're informational
        self.distill_temperature: float = float(distill_temperature)
        self.sparsity_ratio: float = float(sparsity_ratio)

        # ---- Decoder detection ----
        model_type = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "")).lower()
        is_dec = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "is_decoder",
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "is_causal",
                kwargs.get("is_causal", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False)),
            ),
        )
        _mt = (model_type or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        self.lora_rank = int(_cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)))

        # ---- Projections ----
        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            freeze_base=True,
        )

        dropout_p = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "dropout",
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "hidden_dropout_prob",
                _cfg_get(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
            ),
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # HAD-FIX-01: rename trainable scalars with `lora_` prefix so that
        # framework's get_recovery_params recognizes them.
        self.lora_q_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.num_heads, 1))
        self.lora_k_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.num_heads, 1))
        self.lora_temp = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(max(distill_temperature, 1e-2)))

        self.rotary_emb = getattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb", None)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight transfer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. UniversalAdapter
        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 2. GPT-2 Fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v")):
            weight, bias_data = _unpack_weight_bias(src.c_attn)
            if weight is not None:
                if weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[1] == 3 * self.hidden_size:
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
                extracted["q"] = (
                    weight[:split_size, :],
                    bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    (bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None),
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    weight[2 * split_size :, :],
                    bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            w_o, b_o = _unpack_weight_bias(src.c_proj)
            if w_o is not None:
                if (
                    w_o.dim() == 2
                    and w_o.shape[0] != self.hidden_size
                    and w_o.shape[1] == self.hidden_size
                ):
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                extracted["o"] = (w_o, b_o)

        # 3. RoBERTa / BERT nested
        if hasattr(src, "self") and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v")):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query") and extracted["q"][0] is None:
                extracted["q"] = _unpack_weight_bias(s.query)
            if hasattr(s, "key") and extracted["k"][0] is None:
                extracted["k"] = _unpack_weight_bias(s.key)
            if hasattr(s, "value") and extracted["v"][0] is None:
                extracted["v"] = _unpack_weight_bias(s.value)

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            extracted["o"] = _unpack_weight_bias(src.output.dense)

        # 4. Flat modules
        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        w, b = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(src, name))
                        if w is not None:
                            extracted[proj] = (w, b)
                            break

        # ---- Nested encoder detection ----
        cls_name = getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__
        has_out = any(hasattr(src, a) for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj"))
        if hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = False
        elif (
            (
                cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention")
                or (hasattr(src, "query") and not has_out)
            )
            or hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src, "output")
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
        else:
            self.is_nested_encoder = False

        missing = [k for k in ("q", "k", "v") if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.debug("HammingAttention partial extraction; missing: %s", missing)

        # Frobenius check
        for proj_key in ("q", "k", "v"):
            w = extracted[proj_key][0]
            if w is not None:
                n = float(torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item())
                if n < 1e-4:
                    logger.debug("HammingAttention: %s near-zero norm (%.2e)", proj_key, n)
        if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
            n = float(torch.linalg.norm(extracted["o"][0].float()).item())
            if n < 1e-4:
                logger.debug("HammingAttention: out near-zero norm (%.2e)", n)

        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in (
                ("q", self.q_proj),
                ("k", self.k_proj),
                ("v", self.v_proj),
                ("o", self.out_proj),
            ):
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    in_dim = self.all_head_size if proj_key == "o" else self.hidden_size
                    w_oriented = _orient(w, in_dim)
                    target_w = target.weight
                    out_d, in_d = target_w.shape
                    src_out, src_in = w_oriented.shape
                    target_w.data[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)].copy_(
                        w_oriented[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)].to(target_w.dtype)
                    )
                    if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                        b_val = b.data if isinstance(b, nn.Parameter) else b
                        n = min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))
                        target.bias.data[:n].copy_(b_val[:n].to(target.bias.dtype))
                elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    min_d = min(target.weight.shape[0], target.weight.shape[1])
                    target.weight.data.zero_()
                    target.weight.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    if target.bias is not None:
                        target.bias.data.zero_()

                target.freeze_base_weights()

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        key_value_states = kwargs.get("key_value_states", encoder_hidden_states)
        is_cross_attention = key_value_states is not None
        kv_input = key_value_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states

        B, L_q, D = hidden_states.shape
        L_k = kv_input.shape[1]
        H, Dh = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L_q, H, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, L_k, self.num_kv_heads, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, L_k, self.num_kv_heads, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # ---- RoPE ----
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross_attention:
            if isinstance(position_embeddings, (tuple, list)) and len(position_embeddings) >= 2:
                cos, sin = position_embeddings[0], position_embeddings[1]
                Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))

        # ---- GQA ----
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        # ---- Hamming attention scores ----
        # Continuous relaxation of Hamming distance via tanh binarization.
        # Soft-binary: q_b, k_b ∈ (-1, 1)^Dh.
        # Inner product <q_b, k_b> ∈ (-Dh, Dh), so (matmul - Dh) is in (-2Dh, 0).
        # Dividing by 2*sqrt(Dh) normalizes; multiplying by learned temp sharpens.
        q_b = torch.tanh(5.0 * Q * self.lora_q_scale.view(1, H, 1, 1))
        k_b = torch.tanh(5.0 * K * self.lora_k_scale.view(1, H, 1, 1))

        # HAD-FIX-02: ensure temperature is positive (softmax over -Hamming needs T > 0)
        temp = F.softplus(self.lora_temp) + 1e-3

        scores = (
            temp
            * (torch.matmul(q_b, k_b.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) - Dh)
            / (2.0 * math.sqrt(Dh))
        )

        # ---- Masking ----
        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4:
                scores = scores + mask.to(scores.dtype)
            else:
                valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, L_k, device)
                if valid_m is not None:
                    scores = scores.masked_fill((~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)

        # ---- Causal mask ----
        if self.is_decoder and not is_cross_attention and L_q > 1:
            causal_tril = (
                torch.tril(
                    torch.ones((L_q, L_k), device=device, dtype=torch.bool),
                    diagonal=L_k - L_q,
                )
                .unsqueeze(0)
                .unsqueeze(0)
            )
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal_tril, -1e4)

        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        attn = F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).to(dtype)
        attn = torch.nan_to_num(attn, nan=0.0)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)

        # ---- head_mask ----
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            attn = attn * head_mask

        context = torch.matmul(attn, V)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L_q, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(context)

        if output.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
            if output.shape[-1] < hidden_states.shape[-1]:
                output = F.pad(output, (0, hidden_states.shape[-1] - output.shape[-1]))
            else:
                output = output[..., : hidden_states.shape[-1]]

        return output, (attn if output_attentions else None)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("lsh_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class LSHAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any,
        rank: int | None = None,
        rounds: int = 4,
        num_buckets: int = 16,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn

        # LSH-02: robust super().__init__
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        # LSH-10: set self.config explicitly
        self.config = config

        # LSH-03, LSH-13: robust dimension detection (independent of ModelAdapter)
        hidden_size = self._detect_hidden_size(config, source_attn, kwargs)
        num_heads = self._detect_num_heads(config, source_attn, kwargs, hidden_size)
        num_kv_heads = self._detect_num_kv_heads(config, source_attn, kwargs, num_heads)
        head_dim = self._detect_head_dim(config, source_attn, kwargs, hidden_size, num_heads)

        self.hidden_size = int(hidden_size)
        self.num_heads = int(num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = int(num_kv_heads)
        self.head_dim = int(head_dim)
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        # LSH-11: clamp num_buckets
        self.rounds = max(1, int(getattr(config, "lsh_rounds", rounds) if config else rounds))
        self.num_buckets = max(
            2,
            int(getattr(config, "lsh_buckets", num_buckets) if config else num_buckets),
        )

        # Decoder detection
        self.is_decoder = _detect_decoder(config, is_decoder, source_attn)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # LoRA config
        self.lora_rank = int(
            (getattr(config, "lora_rank", None) if config else None)
            or (getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", None) if comp_config else None)
            or lora_rank
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            (getattr(config, "lora_alpha", None) if config else None)
            or (getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", None) if comp_config else None)
            or lora_alpha
        )

        # LSH-08: projection rank
        self.proj_rank = int(
            kwargs.get("proj_rank")
            or (getattr(comp_config, "proj_rank", None) if comp_config else None)
            or (rank if rank is not None else 64)
        )

        # FactorizedLoRALinear projections
        FF = FactorizedLoRALinear
        self.query = FF(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.key = FF(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.value = FF(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = FF(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_dropout_prob",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
                ),
            )
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # LSH-14: per-round random hyperplane projection, normalized per column
        # Shape: (rounds, head_dim, num_buckets // 2)
        half_buckets = max(1, self.num_buckets // 2)
        proj_data = torch.randn(self.rounds, self.head_dim, half_buckets)
        # Normalize each hyperplane (column) to unit L2 norm
        proj_data = F.normalize(proj_data, p=2, dim=1)
        self.register_buffer("proj_matrices", proj_data, persistent=False)

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LSH-13: dimension detection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _detect_hidden_size(config: Any, src: nn.Module | None, kwargs: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        for k in ("hidden_size", "d_model", "n_embd", "dim"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("hidden_size", "d_model", "n_embd", "dim"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for attr in ("out_lin", "out_proj", "o_proj", "dense", "c_proj"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "out_features"):
                    return int(mod.out_features)
            for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "c_attn"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "in_features"):
                    return int(mod.in_features)
        return 768

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_num_heads(
        config: Any, src: nn.Module | None, kwargs: dict[str, Any], hidden_size: int
    ) -> int:
        for k in ("num_heads", "num_attention_heads", "n_heads", "n_head"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("num_attention_heads", "num_heads", "n_heads", "n_head"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for k in ("num_heads", "num_attention_heads", "n_heads"):
                v = getattr(src, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        return max(1, hidden_size // 64)

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_num_kv_heads(
        config: Any, src: nn.Module | None, kwargs: dict[str, Any], num_heads: int
    ) -> int:
        for k in ("num_key_value_heads", "num_kv_heads"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("num_key_value_heads", "num_kv_heads"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            v = getattr(src, "num_key_value_heads", None)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        return num_heads

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_head_dim(
        config: Any,
        src: nn.Module | None,
        kwargs: dict[str, Any],
        hidden_size: int,
        num_heads: int,
    ) -> int:
        v = kwargs.get("head_dim")
        if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
            return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("head_dim", "kv_channels", "d_kv"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight is not None:
                    try:
                        return int(mod.weight.shape[0] // max(1, num_heads))
                    except Exception:
                        pass
        return max(1, hidden_size // max(1, num_heads))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SVD init
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _svd_init(w: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor | None, proj: FactorizedLoRALinear) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = w.detach().float()
            if W.ndim != 2:
                W = W.reshape(W.size(0), -1)

            out_t, in_t = proj.out_features, proj.in_features
            if W.shape[0] != out_t or W.shape[1] != in_t:
                padded = torch.zeros(out_t, in_t, dtype=W.dtype, device=W.device)
                r = min(out_t, W.shape[0])
                c = min(in_t, W.shape[1])
                padded[:r, :c] = W[:r, :c]
                W = padded

            try:
                U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("LSHAttention._svd_init SVD failed: %s", e)
                return

            r = min(proj.rank, U.size(1), Vh.size(0))
            proj.down.weight.data.zero_()
            proj.up.weight.data.zero_()
            proj.down.weight.data[:r, :].copy_(Vh[:r, :].to(proj.down.weight.dtype))
            US = U[:, :r] @ torch.diag(S[:r])
            proj.up.weight.data[:, :r].copy_(US.to(proj.up.weight.dtype))

            if b is not None and proj.up.bias is not None:
                n = min(b.numel(), proj.up.bias.numel())
                proj.up.bias.data[:n].copy_(b[:n].to(proj.up.bias.dtype))
            elif proj.up.bias is not None:
                proj.up.bias.data.zero_()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LSH-07: copy_weights raises on missing
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # Try UniversalAdapter first
        try:
            from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

            for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
                try:
                    w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[proj] = (w, b)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        except Exception:
            pass

        # 1. GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if (
            hasattr(src, "c_attn")
            and src.c_attn is not None
            and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v"))
        ):
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[1] == 3 * self.hidden_size)
                )
                if is_conv:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split, :],
                    c_b[:split] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split : 2 * split, :],
                    c_b[split : 2 * split] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None and extracted["o"][0] is None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. BERT / RoBERTa nested
        elif (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v"))
        ):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query") and extracted["q"][0] is None:
                extracted["q"] = (s.query.weight.data, getattr(s.query, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "key") and extracted["k"][0] is None:
                extracted["k"] = (s.key.weight.data, getattr(s.key, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "value") and extracted["v"][0] is None:
                extracted["v"] = (s.value.weight.data, getattr(s.value, "bias", None))
            if (
                hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
                and extracted["o"][0] is None
            ):
                dense = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (dense.weight.data, getattr(dense, "bias", None))

        # 3. Flat modules (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
        else:
            named_map = {
                "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
                "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
                "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
                "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
            }
            for proj, names in named_map.items():
                if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                    for name in names:
                        if hasattr(src, name):
                            mod = getattr(src, name)
                            if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                                w_data = mod.weight.data
                                if (
                                    proj == "o"
                                    and w_data.ndim == 2
                                    and w_data.shape[0] != self.hidden_size
                                    and w_data.shape[1] == self.hidden_size
                                ):
                                    w_data = w_data.t().contiguous()
                                extracted[proj] = (w_data, getattr(mod, "bias", None))
                                break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"LSHAttention.copy_weights: missing mandatory projections {missing} "
                f"from {cls_name}."
            )

        def _orient(w, in_dim):
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        def _resize_gqa(w, b, out_target):
            if w is None:
                return w, b
            src_out = w.shape[0]
            if src_out == out_target:
                return w, b
            if src_out > out_target and src_out % out_target == 0:
                rep = src_out // out_target
                w = w.view(out_target, rep, -1).mean(dim=1)
                if b is not None:
                    b = b.view(out_target, rep).mean(dim=1)
            elif src_out < out_target and out_target % src_out == 0:
                rep = out_target // src_out
                w = w.repeat(rep, 1)
                if b is not None:
                    b = b.repeat(rep)
            return w, b

        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q, _ = extracted["q"]
            w_k, _ = extracted["k"]
            w_v, _ = extracted["v"]
            w_o, b_o = extracted["o"]

            if w_q is not None:
                self._svd_init(_orient(w_q, self.hidden_size), None, self.query)
            if w_k is not None:
                w_k_o = _orient(w_k, self.hidden_size)
                w_k_res, _ = _resize_gqa(w_k_o, None, self.key.out_features)
                self._svd_init(w_k_res, None, self.key)
            if w_v is not None:
                w_v_o = _orient(w_v, self.hidden_size)
                w_v_res, _ = _resize_gqa(w_v_o, None, self.value.out_features)
                self._svd_init(w_v_res, None, self.value)
            if w_o is not None:
                self._svd_init(_orient(w_o, self.all_head_size), b_o, self.out_proj)
            elif self.is_nested_encoder:
                eye = torch.eye(
                    self.all_head_size,
                    self.all_head_size,
                    device=self.out_proj.down.weight.device,
                    dtype=self.out_proj.down.weight.dtype,
                )
                self._svd_init(eye, None, self.out_proj)

            # Norm verification
            for name, mod in (
                ("query", self.query),
                ("key", self.key),
                ("value", self.value),
                ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
            ):
                try:
                    w_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(mod.weight.float()).item())
                    if w_norm < 1e-4:
                        logger.warning(
                            "LSHAttention: weight '%s' has near-zero norm (%.2e).",
                            name,
                            w_norm,
                        )
                except Exception:
                    pass

            for mod in (self.query, self.key, self.value, self.out_proj):
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

        # Forward sanity check
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                d = next(self.parameters()).device
                dt = self.query.down.weight.dtype
                dummy = torch.randn(1, 8, self.hidden_size, device=d, dtype=dt)
                _ = self.forward(dummy)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("LSHAttention forward sanity check warning: %s", e)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LSH hashing
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _hash(self, vectors: torch.Tensor, proj: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Hash vectors via random hyperplane sign pattern.
        vectors: (B, H, N, d); proj: (d, num_buckets//2)
        Returns: (B, H, N) bucket indices in [0, num_buckets)
        """
        norm = F.normalize(vectors.float(), p=2, dim=-1)
        dots = torch.matmul(norm, proj.float())  # (B, H, N, num_buckets//2)
        signs = (dots >= 0).long()  # (B, H, N, num_buckets//2)
        # Combine sign bits into a single bucket index
        num_planes = signs.shape[-1]
        weights = (2 ** torch.arange(num_planes, device=signs.device)).long()
        bucket = (signs * weights).sum(dim=-1)  # (B, H, N)
        bucket = bucket % self.num_buckets
        return bucket

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LSH-09: chunked LSH attention
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _lsh_attention_chunked(
        self,
        Q: torch.Tensor,  # (B, H, N, d)
        K: torch.Tensor,  # (B, H, N, d)
        V: torch.Tensor,  # (B, H, N, d)
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None,  # (B, N) bool
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        """
        Chunked LSH attention. For each round, compute per-query chunk attention
        over keys in the same bucket. Memory O(B*H*N*C) per round (C=chunk).
        """
        B, H, N, d = Q.shape
        device = Q.device
        dtype = Q.dtype
        scale = d**-0.5

        num_chunks = max(1, (N + 63) // 64)
        chunk_size = (N + num_chunks - 1) // num_chunks

        accum = torch.zeros_like(Q)
        rounds_eff = min(self.rounds, self.proj_matrices.size(0))

        for r in range(rounds_eff):
            proj_r = self.proj_matrices[r].to(device=device, dtype=dtype)
            q_hash = self._hash(Q, proj_r)  # (B, H, N)
            k_hash = self._hash(K, proj_r)

            for c in range(num_chunks):
                c_start = c * chunk_size
                c_end = min(c_start + chunk_size, N)
                Qc = Q[:, :, c_start:c_end]  # (B, H, C, d)
                hq_c = q_hash[:, :, c_start:c_end]  # (B, H, C)

                # bucket match: (B, H, C, N)
                bucket_match = hq_c.unsqueeze(-1) == k_hash.unsqueeze(-2)

                # Mask padding keys
                if valid_mask is not None:
                    kv_mask = valid_mask[:, None, None, :]  # (B, 1, 1, N)
                    bucket_match = bucket_match & kv_mask

                # scores: (B, H, C, N)
                scores_c = (
                    torch.matmul(Qc.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                )

                scores_c = scores_c.masked_fill(~bucket_match, -1e4)
                scores_c = torch.nan_to_num(scores_c, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

                attn_c = F.softmax(scores_c, dim=-1).to(dtype)
                attn_c = torch.nan_to_num(attn_c, nan=0.0)
                attn_c = self.dropout(attn_c)

                accum[:, :, c_start:c_end] = accum[:, :, c_start:c_end] + torch.matmul(attn_c, V)

        accum = accum / float(rounds_eff)
        return accum, None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        # Handle key_value_states via kwargs
        key_value_states = kwargs.get("key_value_states", encoder_hidden_states)
        is_cross = key_value_states is not None
        kv_input = key_value_states if is_cross else hidden_states

        B, N_q, _ = hidden_states.shape
        N_k = kv_input.shape[1]
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        Q = self.query(hidden_states).view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.key(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.value(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # RoPE with past_len offset
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            past_len = 0
            if past_key_value is not None:
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 1:
                    past_len = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
                elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                    past_len = past_key_value.key_cache[0].shape[-2]
            if past_len > 0:
                cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + N_q, :]
                sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + N_q, :]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        # GQA
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        # Valid mask
        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        valid_mask = None
        if eff_mask is not None:
            if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                if eff_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    valid_mask = eff_mask
                elif eff_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    valid_mask = eff_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    valid_mask = eff_mask > 0
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                if eff_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    v = eff_mask
                elif eff_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    v = eff_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    v = eff_mask > 0
                valid_mask = v.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)

        # LSH-15: decoders fall back to exact attention (LSH doesn't naturally support causal)
        use_exact = self.is_decoder or is_cross or N_q <= self.num_buckets

        if use_exact:
            scale = d**-0.5
            scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            if valid_mask is not None:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask[:, None, None, :], -1e4)

            if self.is_decoder and not is_cross:
                causal_tril = (
                    torch.tril(torch.ones((N_q, N_k), device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                )
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal_tril, -1e4)

            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
            attn = torch.nan_to_num(attn, nan=0.0)
            attn = self.dropout(attn)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    hm = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
                else:
                    hm = head_mask
                attn = attn * hm.to(attn.dtype)

            context = torch.matmul(attn, V)
            attn_ret = attn if output_attentions else None
        else:
            # LSH-09: chunked LSH attention
            context, _ = self._lsh_attention_chunked(Q, K, V, valid_mask)
            attn_ret = None
            # Note: LSH path does not produce a single interpretable attention matrix.

        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N_q, self.all_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out.to(dtype), attn_ret

class LoRALinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        freeze_base: bool = False,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        factory_kwargs = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.lora_rank = lora_rank
        self.lora_alpha = lora_alpha
        self.lora_scaling = (lora_alpha / lora_rank) if lora_rank > 0 else 1.0
        self.freeze_base = freeze_base

        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_features, in_features), **factory_kwargs))
        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, **factory_kwargs))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((lora_rank, in_features), **factory_kwargs))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_features, lora_rank), **factory_kwargs))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        self.reset_parameters()
        if freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        if self.bias is not None:
            fan_in, _ = nn.init._calculate_fan_in_and_fan_out(self.weight)
            bound = 1 / math.sqrt(fan_in) if fan_in > 0 else 0
            nn.init.uniform_(self.bias, -bound, bound)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None:
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_B is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        self.weight.requires_grad = False
        if self.bias is not None:
            self.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            if self.lora_A is not None:
                self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            if self.lora_B is not None:
                self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        target_dtype = x.dtype
        w = self.weight.to(target_dtype) if self.weight.dtype != target_dtype else self.weight
        b = (
            self.bias.to(target_dtype)
            if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != target_dtype)
            else self.bias
        )
        out = F.linear(x, w, b)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            a = self.lora_A.to(target_dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != target_dtype else self.lora_A
            bb = self.lora_B.to(target_dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != target_dtype else self.lora_B
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, a), bb) * self.lora_scaling
            out = out + lora_out
        return out.to(target_dtype)

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    target_dtype = x.dtype
    w = self.weight.to(target_dtype) if self.weight.dtype != target_dtype else self.weight
    b = (
        self.bias.to(target_dtype)
        if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != target_dtype)
        else self.bias
    )
    out = F.linear(x, w, b)
    if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        a = self.lora_A.to(target_dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != target_dtype else self.lora_A
        bb = self.lora_B.to(target_dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != target_dtype else self.lora_B
        lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, a), bb) * self.lora_scaling
        out = out + lora_out
    return out.to(target_dtype)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.
