@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("moba_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class MoBAAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    MoBA: Mixture of Block Attention for Long-Context Modeling.

    For each query block, we compute block-level centroid scores, select the
    top-k most relevant key blocks, and run dense attention within the
    selected blocks (plus the causal-visible diagonal block).
    """

    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    triton_kernel: str = "sparse_attn"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any,
        block_size: int = 64,
        top_k: int = 4,
        rank: int | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        # MB-FIX-03
        super().__init__(config, rank if rank is not None else 32)

        # MB-FIX-04: config None-safe
        if config is None:

            class _Cfg:
                pass

            config = _Cfg()
            config.hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
            config.num_attention_heads = kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
            config.head_dim = kwargs.get("head_dim", 64)
            config.model_type = kwargs.get("model_type", "")

        self.num_heads = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                getattr(config, "num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 4)),
            )
        )
        self.hidden_size = int(getattr(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)))
        cfg_hd = getattr(config, "head_dim", None)
        self.head_dim = (
            int(cfg_hd)
            if cfg_hd is not None
            else max(1, self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads))
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim

        cfg_kv = getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
        if cfg_kv is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv)
        elif kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(kwargs["num_kv_heads"])
        else:
            self.num_kv_heads = self.num_heads

        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))
        self.kv_repeat = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.block_size = int(block_size)
        self.top_k = int(top_k)
        self.lora_rank = int(lora_rank)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            _mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            self.is_decoder = bool(
                getattr(config, "is_decoder", False) or getattr(config, "causal", False)
            ) or any(
                _mt.startswith(p)
                for p in (
                    "gpt",
                    "qwen",
                    "llama",
                    "mistral",
                    "falcon",
                    "bloom",
                    "opt",
                    "gemma",
                    "phi",
                    "olmo",
                    "mpt",
                    "stablelm",
                )
            )

        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        # ---- MB-FIX-01: output scale (initialized to 1.0) ----
        self.output_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1))

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # ---- projections ----
        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )

        # MB-FIX-11: storage accounting
        self._orig_storage_bytes = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes = 0
        self.triton_kwargs = {"block_size": self.block_size, "eps": 1e-6}

        pretrained_attn = kwargs.get("pretrained_attn")
        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    # ---- MB-FIX-07: property aliases ----
    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        # Track original storage
        try:
            for p in src.parameters():
                self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. UniversalAdapter
        for proj in ("q", "k", "v", "o"):
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        # 2. GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            c_attn = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn, "base_layer", c_attn)
            cw = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn, "weight", None))
            if cw is not None:
                cw = cw.data.clone()
                if cw.dim() == 2 and cw.shape[0] < cw.shape[1]:
                    cw = cw.t().contiguous()
                cb = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn, "bias", None))
                cb_data = cb.data.clone() if cb is not None else None
                split = cw.shape[0] // 3
                extracted["q"] = (
                    cw[:split],
                    cb_data[:split] if cb_data is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    cw[split : 2 * split],
                    cb_data[split : 2 * split] if cb_data is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    cw[2 * split :],
                    cb_data[2 * split :] if cb_data is not None else None,
                )

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            cp = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(cp, "base_layer", cp)
            ow = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(cp, "weight", None))
            if ow is not None:
                ow = ow.data.clone()
                if ow.dim() == 2 and ow.shape[0] < ow.shape[1]:
                    ow = ow.t().contiguous()
                bo = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(cp, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (ow, bo.data.clone() if bo is not None else None)

        # 3. RoBERTa / BERT nested (MB-FIX-02: pass .data, not module)
        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query") and extracted["q"][0] is None:
                q_l = getattr(s.query, "base_layer", s.query)
                extracted["q"] = (
                    q_l.weight.data,
                    q_l.bias.data if getattr(q_l, "bias", None) is not None else None,
                )
            if hasattr(s, "key") and extracted["k"][0] is None:
                k_l = getattr(s.key, "base_layer", s.key)
                extracted["k"] = (
                    k_l.weight.data,
                    k_l.bias.data if getattr(k_l, "bias", None) is not None else None,
                )
            if hasattr(s, "value") and extracted["v"][0] is None:
                v_l = getattr(s.value, "base_layer", s.value)
                extracted["v"] = (
                    v_l.weight.data,
                    v_l.bias.data if getattr(v_l, "bias", None) is not None else None,
                )

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            d = getattr(src.output.dense, "base_layer", src.output.dense)
            extracted["o"] = (
                d.weight.data,
                d.bias.data if getattr(d, "bias", None) is not None else None,
            )

        # 4. Modern decoders / DistilBERT
        named_map = {
            "q": ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"),
            "k": ("k_proj", "k_lin", "key"),
            "v": ("v_proj", "v_lin", "value"),
            "o": ("o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"),
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        m = getattr(src, name)
                        w = getattr(m, "weight", None)
                        if w is not None and hasattr(w, "data"):
                            b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                w.data,
                                b.data if b is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # MB-FIX-06: nested encoder detection after all extraction
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, a)
            for a in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            or cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention", "XLMRobertaSelfAttention")
            or cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention")
            and not has_out_proj
            or cls_name.endswith("Attention")
            and not has_out_proj
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        missing = [k for k in ("q", "k", "v") if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.warning(
                "MoBA copy_weights: missing %s; proceeding with initialized weights.",
                missing,
            )

        # Frobenius norm checks
        for k_ in ("q", "k", "v"):
            w = extracted[k_][0]
            if w is not None:
                n = torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item()
                if n < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning("MoBA: %s weight near-zero norm (%.2e)", k_, n)
        if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
            n = torch.linalg.norm(extracted["o"][0].float()).item()
            if n < 1e-4:
                logger.warning("MoBA: o weight near-zero norm (%.2e)", n)

        def _orient(w, in_dim):
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        # ---- Copy ----
        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in (
                ("q", self.q_proj),
                ("k", self.k_proj),
                ("v", self.v_proj),
                ("o", self.out_proj),
            ):
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    in_dim = self.all_head_size if proj_key == "o" else self.hidden_size
                    w_oriented = _orient(w, in_dim)
                    target_w = target.weight
                    out_d, in_d = target_w.shape
                    src_out, src_in = w_oriented.shape

                    if proj_key in ("k", "v"):
                        if src_out == out_d:
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented)
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b)
                        elif src_out > out_d and src_out % out_d == 0:
                            rep = src_out // out_d
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented.view(out_d, rep, -1).mean(dim=1))
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b.view(out_d, rep).mean(dim=1))
                        elif src_out < out_d and out_d % src_out == 0:
                            rep = out_d // src_out
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented.repeat(rep, 1))
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b.repeat(rep))
                        else:
                            m0, m1 = min(out_d, src_out), min(in_d, src_in)
                            target_w.data[:m0, :m1].copy_(w_oriented[:m0, :m1])
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                mb = min(target.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                                target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb])
                    else:
                        m0, m1 = min(out_d, src_out), min(in_d, src_in)
                        target_w.data[:m0, :m1].copy_(w_oriented[:m0, :m1])
                        if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                            mb = min(target.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                            target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb])
                elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    min_d = min(target.weight.shape[0], target.weight.shape[1])
                    target.weight.data.zero_()
                    target.weight.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    if target.bias is not None:
                        target.bias.data.zero_()

                target.freeze_base_weights()

        # Track compressed storage
        try:
            for m in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj):
                for p in m.parameters():
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        # ---- is_decoder override ----
        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)
        elif (
            cls_name in ("GPT2Attention", "GPT2Block")
            or hasattr(src, "c_attn")
            or "gpt" in cls_name.lower()
        ):
            self.is_decoder = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        L_kv = kv_input.shape[1]

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, L_kv, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, L_kv, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        if self.num_kv_heads < self.num_heads:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        is_causal = bool(self.is_decoder and not is_cross and L > 1)
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask

        # ---- Normalize mask to bool (B, L_kv) ----
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None
        if eff_mask is not None:
            if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                if eff_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    valid_mask = eff_mask
                elif eff_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    valid_mask = eff_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    valid_mask = eff_mask > 0
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 3:
                m = eff_mask
                if m.dtype == torch.bool:
                    valid_mask = m.squeeze(1)
                elif m.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    valid_mask = (m > -1e4).squeeze(1)
                else:
                    valid_mask = (m > 0).squeeze(1)
            elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                m = eff_mask
                if m.dtype == torch.bool:
                    vv = m
                elif m.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    vv = m > -1e4
                else:
                    vv = m > 0
                valid_mask = vv.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)

            if valid_mask is not None and valid_mask.shape[-1] != L_kv:
                valid_mask = None  # shape mismatch → ignore

        # ---- Fast path: short sequence or cross-attention ----
        if self.block_size >= L or is_cross:
            if valid_mask is None and head_mask is None and not output_attentions:
                try:
                    _qs = Q.half() if Q.dtype == torch.float32 else Q
                    _ks = K.half() if K.dtype == torch.float32 else K
                    _vs = V.half() if V.dtype == torch.float32 else V
                    _is_causal = self.is_decoder and not is_cross and L > 1
                    _sdpa_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                        _qs,
                        _ks,
                        _vs,
                        is_causal=_is_causal,
                        dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                    )
                    _sdpa_out = (
                        _sdpa_out.to(hidden_states.dtype)
                        .transpose(1, 2)
                        .contiguous()
                        .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
                    )
                    _sdpa_out = torch.nan_to_num(_sdpa_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
                    if self.is_nested_encoder:
                        return _sdpa_out * self.output_scale, None
                    out = self.out_proj(_sdpa_out)
                    return out * self.output_scale, None
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # Dense manual
            scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            if is_causal:
                causal_mask = (
                    torch.triu(
                        torch.full((L, L_kv), float("-inf"), device=device, dtype=torch.float32),
                        diagonal=1,
                    )
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                )
                scores = scores + causal_mask

            if valid_mask is not None:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask[:, None, None, :], -1e4)

            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            attn_probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
            attn_probs = torch.nan_to_num(attn_probs, nan=0.0)
            attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)

            # MB-FIX-09: head_mask shape
            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.shape[0], head_mask.shape[1], 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(1)  # interpret as (B, H, 1)
                attn_probs = attn_probs * head_mask.to(attn_probs.dtype)

            context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, V)
            out = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        else:
            # ---- Block-sparse path ----
            pad = (self.block_size - L % self.block_size) % self.block_size
            if pad > 0:
                Q_pad = F.pad(Q, (0, 0, 0, pad))
                K_pad = F.pad(K, (0, 0, 0, pad))
                V_pad = F.pad(V, (0, 0, 0, pad))
                if valid_mask is not None:
                    valid_mask_padded = F.pad(valid_mask, (0, pad), value=False)
                else:
                    valid_mask_padded = torch.ones(B, L + pad, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)
            else:
                Q_pad, K_pad, V_pad = Q, K, V
                if valid_mask is not None:
                    valid_mask_padded = valid_mask
                else:
                    valid_mask_padded = torch.ones(B, L, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

            Lp = L + pad
            nb = Lp // self.block_size
            bs = self.block_size

            Qb = Q_pad.view(B, H, nb, bs, d)
            Kb = K_pad.view(B, H, nb, bs, d)
            Vb = V_pad.view(B, H, nb, bs, d)

            # ---- Block-level scores ----
            Q_mean = Qb.mean(dim=3)
            K_mean = Kb.mean(dim=3)
            block_scores = (
                torch.matmul(Q_mean.float(), K_mean.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            )

            # MB-FIX-08: block-0 preservation
            # - For bidirectional encoders, block 0 (CLS) should always be a candidate.
            # - For causal decoders, causality is enforced per-position below, so we
            #   don't need to force block 0; a query at block i > 0 would never see
            #   block 0 through causal masking.
            if not is_causal:
                block_scores_for_topk = block_scores.clone()
                block_scores_for_topk[:, :, :, 0] = block_scores_for_topk[:, :, :, 0] + 1e4
            else:
                block_scores_for_topk = block_scores.clone()
                # For causal: a query in block i can only attend to blocks j ≤ i.
                # Mask blocks j > i to -inf.
                causal_block = torch.triu(
                    torch.full((nb, nb), float("-inf"), device=device, dtype=torch.float32),
                    diagonal=1,
                )
                block_scores_for_topk = block_scores_for_topk + causal_block.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(
                    0
                )

            top_k = min(self.top_k, nb)
            _, top_idx = block_scores_for_topk.topk(top_k, dim=-1)  # (B, H, nb, top_k)

            # MB-FIX-05: valid block mask reshaped correctly
            valid_b_exp = valid_mask_padded.view(B, 1, nb, bs).expand(-1, H, -1, -1)

            out_blocks = torch.zeros_like(Qb)
            for i in range(nb):
                idx = top_idx[:, :, i, :]  # (B, H, top_k)
                idx_exp = idx.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, bs, d)
                K_sel = torch.gather(Kb, 2, idx_exp).view(B, H, top_k * bs, d)
                V_sel = torch.gather(Vb, 2, idx_exp).view(B, H, top_k * bs, d)

                idx_mask_exp = idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, bs)
                k_valid = torch.gather(valid_b_exp, 2, idx_mask_exp).reshape(B, H, 1, top_k * bs)

                Q_i = Qb[:, :, i]  # (B, H, bs, d)
                scores_i = (
                    torch.matmul(Q_i.float(), K_sel.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                )
                scores_i = scores_i.masked_fill(~k_valid, -1e4)

                if is_causal:
                    q_pos = (i * bs + torch.arange(bs, device=device)).view(1, 1, bs, 1)
                    # absolute key positions for each of the top_k blocks
                    k_offsets = idx.unsqueeze(-1) * bs + torch.arange(bs, device=device).view(
                        1, 1, 1, bs
                    )
                    k_pos = k_offsets.reshape(B, H, 1, top_k * bs)
                    invalid = k_pos > q_pos
                    scores_i = scores_i.masked_fill(invalid, -1e4)

                scores_i = torch.nan_to_num(scores_i, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
                attn_i = F.softmax(scores_i, dim=-1).to(dtype)
                attn_i = torch.nan_to_num(attn_i, nan=0.0)
                attn_i = self.dropout(attn_i)
                out_blocks[:, :, i] = torch.matmul(attn_i, V_sel)

            out_pad = out_blocks.view(B, H, Lp, d)[:, :, :L, :]
            out = out_pad.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)

        out = torch.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            final_out = out
        else:
            final_out = self.out_proj(out)

        # MB-FIX-01: apply output_scale
        final_out = final_out * self.output_scale

        return final_out, None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        def _sz(p):
            if p is None:
                return 0
            if isinstance(p, (nn.Parameter, torch.Tensor)):
                return p.numel() * p.element_size()
            return 0

        out = {}
        for name, m in (
            ("q_proj", self.q_proj),
            ("k_proj", self.k_proj),
            ("v_proj", self.v_proj),
            ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
        ):
            out[name] = sum(_sz(p) for p in m.parameters())
        out["output_scale"] = _sz(self.output_scale)
        out["total"] = sum(out.values())
        return out

MoBA: Mixture of Block Attention for Long-Context Modeling.

For each query block, we compute block-level centroid scores, select the top-k most relevant key blocks, and run dense attention within the selected blocks (plus the causal-visible diagonal block).

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("moe_deltakv_paged", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class MoEDeltaKVPagedAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    MoE-DeltaKV-Paged Hybrid Attention.

    Two execution paths:
      * Full dense attention when `num_pages <= 1` (page_size >= seq_len)
        OR when `enable_moe=False`.
      * Paged MoE attention otherwise.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        page_size: int = 16,
        k_experts: int = 4,
        svd_rank: int = 32,
        q_bits: int = 4,
        max_delta_error: float = 0.35,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        enable_moe: bool = True,
        enable_deltakv: bool = True,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()

        if config is None:

            class _Cfg:
                pass

            config = _Cfg()
            config.hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
            config.num_attention_heads = kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
            config.head_dim = kwargs.get("head_dim", 64)
            config.model_type = kwargs.get("model_type", "")
            config.is_decoder = kwargs.get("is_decoder", False)

        # ---- dim inference (MDP-FIX-14) ----
        self.hidden_size = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "hidden_size",
                getattr(config, "d_model", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)),
            )
        )
        self.num_heads = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                getattr(config, "num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)),
            )
        )
        self.head_dim = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "head_dim",
                kwargs.get("head_dim", self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads)),
            )
        )

        cfg_kv = getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
        if cfg_kv is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv)
        elif kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(kwargs["num_kv_heads"])
        else:
            self.num_kv_heads = self.num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        # ---- paging config (MDP-FIX-01, MDP-FIX-12) ----
        self.page_size = max(4, int(page_size))
        self.k_experts = max(1, int(k_experts))
        self.svd_rank = max(4, int(svd_rank))
        self.q_bits = int(q_bits)
        if self.q_bits not in (2, 4, 8):
            self.q_bits = 4
        self.max_delta_error = float(max_delta_error)

        # ---- flags ----
        self.enable_moe = bool(enable_moe)
        self.enable_deltakv = bool(enable_deltakv)
        self.lora_rank = max(0, int(lora_rank))
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_causal = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            _mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            _dec_pfx = (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
            self.is_causal = bool(
                getattr(config, "is_causal", False)
                or getattr(config, "is_decoder", False)
                or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)
                or kwargs.get("is_decoder", False)
                or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _dec_pfx)
            )
        self.is_decoder = self.is_causal
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # ---- projections ----
        self.query = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.key = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.value = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )

        # ---- page router (identity init so it preserves original attention) ----
        self.W_rg = nn.Linear(self.head_dim, self.head_dim, bias=False)
        with torch.no_grad():
            nn.init.eye_(self.W_rg.weight)

        # ---- auxiliary load-balancing loss as a buffer (MDP-FIX-03) ----
        self.register_buffer("aux_loss", torch.zeros(1), persistent=False)

        # ---- delta-KV reconstruction telemetry ----
        self.register_buffer("delta_kv_error_k", torch.zeros(1), persistent=False)
        self.register_buffer("delta_kv_error_v", torch.zeros(1), persistent=False)

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_dropout_prob",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
                ),
            )
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # ---- storage accounting (MDP-FIX-07) ----
        self._orig_storage_bytes: int = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes: int = 0
        self._source_id: int | None = None

        src = pretrained_attn or kwargs.get("old_attn")
        if src is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def q_proj(self):
        return self.query

    @property
    def k_proj(self):
        return self.key

    @property
    def v_proj(self):
        return self.value

    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.query

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.key

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.value

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return {"orig": self._orig_storage_bytes, "comp": self._comp_storage_bytes}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights (MDP-FIX-05, MDP-FIX-09)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        # Idempotency
        if self._source_id is not None and id(src) == self._source_id:
            return

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # --- 1. UniversalAdapter ---
        for proj in ("q", "k", "v", "o"):
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # --- 2. GPT-2 fused c_attn ---
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v")):
            try:
                mod_c = src.c_attn
                base_c = getattr(mod_c, "base_layer", mod_c)
                weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(mod_c, "weight", None))
                if weight is not None:
                    weight = weight.data.clone()
                    bias = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(mod_c, "bias", None))
                    bias_data = bias.data.clone() if bias is not None else None
                    cls_c = mod_c.__class__.__name__
                    base_cls = base_c.__class__.__name__
                    if (
                        hasattr(mod_c, "nf")
                        or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                        or "Conv1D" in cls_c
                        or "Conv1D" in base_cls
                        or (weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[0] < weight.shape[1])
                    ):
                        weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                    split = weight.shape[0] // 3
                    extracted["q"] = (
                        weight[:split],
                        bias_data[:split] if bias_data is not None else None,
                    )
                    extracted["k"] = (
                        weight[split : 2 * split],
                        bias_data[split : 2 * split] if bias_data is not None else None,
                    )
                    extracted["v"] = (
                        weight[2 * split :],
                        bias_data[2 * split :] if bias_data is not None else None,
                    )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("GPT-2 c_attn extraction failed: %s", e)

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            try:
                mod_o = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(mod_o, "base_layer", mod_o)
                w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(mod_o, "weight", None))
                if w_o is not None:
                    w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                    cls_o = mod_o.__class__.__name__
                    base_cls_o = base_o.__class__.__name__
                    if (
                        hasattr(mod_o, "nf")
                        or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                        or "Conv1D" in cls_o
                        or "Conv1D" in base_cls_o
                        or (w_o.dim() == 2 and w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1])
                    ):
                        w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                    b_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(mod_o, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        w_o,
                        b_o.data.clone() if b_o is not None else None,
                    )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("GPT-2 c_proj extraction failed: %s", e)

        # --- 3. BERT / RoBERTa nested ---
        if hasattr(src, "self") and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v")):
            try:
                s = src.self
                if hasattr(s, "query") and extracted["q"][0] is None:
                    extracted["q"] = (
                        s.query.weight.data.clone(),
                        s.query.bias.data.clone() if s.query.bias is not None else None,
                    )
                if hasattr(s, "key") and extracted["k"][0] is None:
                    extracted["k"] = (
                        s.key.weight.data.clone(),
                        s.key.bias.data.clone() if s.key.bias is not None else None,
                    )
                if hasattr(s, "value") and extracted["v"][0] is None:
                    extracted["v"] = (
                        s.value.weight.data.clone(),
                        s.value.bias.data.clone() if s.value.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Nested self extraction failed: %s", e)

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            try:
                dense = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    dense.weight.data.clone(),
                    dense.bias.data.clone() if dense.bias is not None else None,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("output.dense extraction failed: %s", e)

        # --- 4. Flat modules ---
        named_map = {
            "q": ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"),
            "k": ("k_proj", "k_lin", "key"),
            "v": ("v_proj", "v_lin", "value"),
            "o": ("o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"),
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        try:
                            layer = getattr(src, name)
                            if hasattr(layer, "weight"):
                                extracted[proj] = (
                                    layer.weight.data.clone(),
                                    (
                                        layer.bias.data.clone()
                                        if getattr(layer, "bias", None) is not None
                                        else None
                                    ),
                                )
                                break
                        except Exception:
                            continue

        # --- 5. Nested encoder detection (MDP-FIX-05) ---
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, a)
            for a in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            or cls_name
            in (
                "BertSelfAttention",
                "RobertaSelfAttention",
                "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                "ElectraSelfAttention",
            )
        ):
            is_nested = True
        elif cls_name in ("DistilBertAttention", "DistilBertSABlock"):
            # DistilBERT nests attention inside a container but has an out_lin
            is_nested = True
        elif (
            cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention")
            and not has_out_proj
            or cls_name.endswith("Attention")
            and not has_out_proj
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        # --- 6. Missing check ---
        missing = [k for k in ("q", "k", "v") if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.warning("MoEDeltaKVPaged: missing %s; aborting copy.", missing)
            return

        # --- 7. Storage tracking ---
        try:
            for p in src.parameters():
                self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        # --- 8. Copy into projections ---
        with torch.no_grad():
            # Q, K, V
            for proj_key, target in (
                ("q", self.query),
                ("k", self.key),
                ("v", self.value),
            ):
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    continue
                in_dim = self.hidden_size
                out_dim = target.weight.shape[0]

                if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                    w = w.t().contiguous()

                # GQA expansion (repeat)
                if w.shape[0] < out_dim and out_dim % w.shape[0] == 0:
                    rep = out_dim // w.shape[0]
                    w = w.repeat(rep, 1)
                    if b is not None:
                        b = b.repeat(rep)
                elif w.shape[0] > out_dim and w.shape[0] % out_dim == 0:
                    rep = w.shape[0] // out_dim
                    w = w.view(out_dim, rep, -1).mean(dim=1)
                    if b is not None:
                        b = b.view(out_dim, rep).mean(dim=1)

                target.weight.data.zero_()
                out_rows = min(out_dim, w.shape[0])
                in_cols = min(in_dim, w.shape[1])
                target.weight.data[:out_rows, :in_cols].copy_(
                    w[:out_rows, :in_cols].to(target.weight.device, target.weight.dtype)
                )
                if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                    mb = min(target.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target.bias.data.zero_()
                    target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.device, target.bias.dtype))

            # Output projection
            w_o, b_o = extracted["o"]
            if w_o is not None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
                in_dim = self.all_head_size
                out_dim = self.hidden_size
                if w_o.dim() == 2 and w_o.shape[1] != in_dim and w_o.shape[0] == in_dim:
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                self.out_proj.weight.data.zero_()
                out_rows = min(out_dim, w_o.shape[0])
                in_cols = min(in_dim, w_o.shape[1])
                self.out_proj.weight.data[:out_rows, :in_cols].copy_(
                    w_o[:out_rows, :in_cols].to(
                        self.out_proj.weight.device, self.out_proj.weight.dtype
                    )
                )
                if b_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    mb = min(self.out_proj.bias.numel(), b_o.numel())
                    self.out_proj.bias.data.zero_()
                    self.out_proj.bias.data[:mb].copy_(
                        b_o[:mb].to(self.out_proj.bias.device, self.out_proj.bias.dtype)
                    )
            elif self.is_nested_encoder:
                # Nested encoder: out_proj is bypassed in forward.
                pass

            # Freeze base weights
            for m in (self.query, self.key, self.value, self.out_proj):
                m.freeze_base_weights()

        # --- 9. Storage accounting ---
        try:
            for m in (self.query, self.key, self.value, self.out_proj):
                for p in m.parameters():
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        # --- 10. is_decoder override ---
        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)
            self.is_causal = self.is_decoder

        self._source_id = id(src)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Paged MoE block attention (executed when num_pages >= 2)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _paged_moe_attention(
        self,
        q: torch.Tensor,
        k_hat: torch.Tensor,
        v_hat: torch.Tensor,
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, H, N, d = q.shape
        page_size = self.page_size
        num_pages = (N + page_size - 1) // page_size
        pad_len = num_pages * page_size - N

        if pad_len > 0:
            q_pad = F.pad(q, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))
            k_pad = F.pad(k_hat, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))
            v_pad = F.pad(v_hat, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))
            valid_pad = F.pad(valid_mask, (0, pad_len), value=False)
        else:
            q_pad, k_pad, v_pad, valid_pad = q, k_hat, v_hat, valid_mask

        padded_N = num_pages * page_size

        # ---- Blockify ----
        q_blocks = q_pad.view(B, H, num_pages, page_size, d)
        k_blocks = k_pad.view(B, H, num_pages, page_size, d)
        v_blocks = v_pad.view(B, H, num_pages, page_size, d)

        valid_blocks = valid_pad.view(B, 1, num_pages, page_size).float()

        # ---- Page-level pooling ----
        valid_sum = valid_blocks.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1.0)
        q_bar = (q_blocks * valid_blocks.unsqueeze(-1)).sum(dim=3) / valid_sum
        k_bar = (k_blocks * valid_blocks.unsqueeze(-1)).sum(dim=3) / valid_sum

        # ---- Routing ----
        router_logits = torch.matmul(
            self.W_rg(q_bar), k_bar.transpose(-1, -2).contiguous()
        ) / math.sqrt(d)

        # Page validity: at least one valid token in the page
        page_valid = valid_pad.view(B, 1, num_pages, page_size).any(dim=-1)  # (B,1,num_pages)
        router_logits = router_logits.masked_fill(~page_valid.unsqueeze(2), float("-inf"))

        # Causal masking of pages
        if self.is_causal:
            causal_pages = torch.tril(
                torch.ones(num_pages, num_pages, device=q.device, dtype=torch.bool)
            )
            router_logits = router_logits.masked_fill(~causal_pages, float("-inf"))

        router_logits = torch.nan_to_num(router_logits, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        # Ensure page 0 always selected (attention sink / CLS protection)
        router_logits_for_topk = router_logits.clone()
        if self.is_causal:
            # Only bias page 0 for queries whose page index >= 0 (always true)
            router_logits_for_topk[:, :, :, 0] = router_logits_for_topk[:, :, :, 0] + 1e4
        else:
            router_logits_for_topk[:, :, :, 0] = router_logits_for_topk[:, :, :, 0] + 1e4

        k_experts = min(self.k_experts, num_pages)
        _, topk_indices = torch.topk(router_logits_for_topk, k_experts, dim=-1)
        topk_logits = torch.gather(router_logits, -1, topk_indices)
        g_j = F.softmax(topk_logits, dim=-1)  # (B,H,num_pages,k)

        # ---- Auxiliary loss (load balancing) ----
        router_probs = F.softmax(router_logits, dim=-1)  # (B,H,num_pages,num_pages)
        expert_mask = F.one_hot(
            topk_indices, num_classes=num_pages
        ).float()  # (B,H,num_pages,k,num_pages)
        density = expert_mask.sum(dim=-2).mean(dim=(0, 1, 2))  # (num_pages,)
        prob_mean = router_probs.mean(dim=(0, 1, 2))  # (num_pages,)
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.aux_loss.copy_(num_pages * torch.sum(density * prob_mean).detach())

        # ---- Gather selected pages ----
        expanded_idx = topk_indices.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, -1, page_size, d)
        k_blocks_exp = k_blocks.unsqueeze(2).expand(-1, -1, num_pages, -1, -1, -1)
        v_blocks_exp = v_blocks.unsqueeze(2).expand(-1, -1, num_pages, -1, -1, -1)
        k_gathered = torch.gather(
            k_blocks_exp, dim=3, index=expanded_idx
        )  # (B,H,num_pages,k,page_size,d)
        v_gathered = torch.gather(v_blocks_exp, dim=3, index=expanded_idx)

        # ---- Gather validity mask ----
        valid_blocks_exp = valid_blocks.unsqueeze(2).expand(-1, -1, num_pages, -1, -1)
        exp_valid_idx = topk_indices.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, -1, page_size)
        valid_blocks_exp_h = valid_blocks_exp.expand(-1, H, -1, -1, -1)
        k_valid = torch.gather(
            valid_blocks_exp_h, dim=3, index=exp_valid_idx
        )  # (B,H,num_pages,k,page_size)

        # ---- Per-expert attention ----
        q_exp = q_blocks.unsqueeze(3)  # (B,H,num_pages,1,page_size,d)
        attn_scores = torch.matmul(q_exp, k_gathered.transpose(-1, -2).contiguous()) / math.sqrt(d)
        attn_scores = attn_scores.masked_fill(~k_valid.unsqueeze(-2), -1e4)
        attn_scores = torch.nan_to_num(attn_scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        attn_probs = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1)
        attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)

        context_per_expert = torch.matmul(attn_probs, v_gathered)  # (B,H,num_pages,k,page_size,d)

        # ---- Mix experts with gate weights ----
        g_j_exp = g_j.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)  # (B,H,num_pages,k,1,1)
        context = (context_per_expert * g_j_exp).sum(dim=3)  # (B,H,num_pages,page_size,d)

        # ---- Reshape to (B,H,padded_N,d) ----
        context = context.reshape(B, H, padded_N, d)

        return context, attn_probs

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:

        if not hidden_states.is_contiguous():
            hidden_states = hidden_states.contiguous()

        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        H_kv = self.num_kv_heads
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        kv_len = kv_input.shape[1]

        # ---- Projections ----
        q = self.query(hidden_states).view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k = self.key(kv_input).view(B, kv_len, H_kv, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = self.value(kv_input).view(B, kv_len, H_kv, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # ---- RoPE ----
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # ---- GQA ----
        if H_kv != H:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        # ---- Delta-KV compression (MDP-FIX-02) ----
        if self.enable_deltakv and kv_len > 1:
            k_hat, v_hat, err_k, err_v = simulate_paged_delta_kv(
                k,
                v,
                rank=self.svd_rank,
                bits=self.q_bits,
                max_delta_error=self.max_delta_error,
            )
            with torch.no_grad():
                self.delta_kv_error_k.copy_(torch.tensor(err_k, device=device))
                self.delta_kv_error_v.copy_(torch.tensor(err_v, device=device))
        else:
            k_hat, v_hat = k, v

        # ---- Mask normalization ----
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    valid_mask = attention_mask
                elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    valid_mask = attention_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    valid_mask = attention_mask > 0
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
                m = attention_mask
                valid_mask = (m > -1e4).squeeze(1) if m.dtype.is_floating_point else m.squeeze(1)
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                m = attention_mask
                v_bool = (
                    m
                    if m.dtype == torch.bool
                    else (m > -1e4 if m.dtype.is_floating_point else m > 0)
                )
                valid_mask = v_bool.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
            if valid_mask is not None and valid_mask.shape[-1] != kv_len:
                valid_mask = None

        if valid_mask is None:
            valid_mask = torch.ones(B, kv_len, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

        # ---- Decide execution path ----
        num_pages = (kv_len + self.page_size - 1) // self.page_size
        use_paged = self.enable_moe and num_pages > 1 and not is_cross

        if use_paged:
            context, attn_probs = self._paged_moe_attention(q, k_hat, v_hat, valid_mask)
        else:
            # ---- Dense path ----
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k_hat.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            if self.is_causal and not is_cross and N > 1:
                diag = 1 + max(0, kv_len - N)
                causal = (
                    torch.triu(
                        torch.full(
                            (N, kv_len),
                            float("-inf"),
                            device=device,
                            dtype=torch.float32,
                        ),
                        diagonal=diag,
                    )
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                    .unsqueeze(0)
                )
                scores = scores + causal

            scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask[:, None, None, :], -1e4)
            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

            attn_probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
            attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)
            context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, v_hat)

            with torch.no_grad():
                self.aux_loss.zero_()

        # ---- head_mask ----
        if head_mask is not None:
            hm = head_mask.to(context.dtype)
            if hm.dim() == 1:
                hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 2:
                hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], hm.shape[1], 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 3:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
            context = context * hm

        # ---- Reshape to (B, N, all_head_size) ----
        context = context[:, :, :N, :] if context.shape[2] != N else context
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        # ---- Output projection ----
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(context)

        # MDP-FIX-13: always return a 2-tuple with attn = tensor or None
        attn_ret = attn_probs if output_attentions else None
        return out, attn_ret

MoE-DeltaKV-Paged Hybrid Attention.

Two execution paths: * Full dense attention when num_pages <= 1 (page_size >= seq_len) OR when enable_moe=False. * Paged MoE attention otherwise.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def copy_weights(self,
src: Any) ‑> None
Expand source code 
def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
    if src is None:
        return

    from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

    # Idempotency
    if self._source_id is not None and id(src) == self._source_id:
        return

    extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
        "q": (None, None),
        "k": (None, None),
        "v": (None, None),
        "o": (None, None),
    }

    # --- 1. UniversalAdapter ---
    for proj in ("q", "k", "v", "o"):
        try:
            w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
            if w is not None:
                extracted[proj] = (w, b)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # --- 2. GPT-2 fused c_attn ---
    if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v")):
        try:
            mod_c = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(mod_c, "base_layer", mod_c)
            weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(mod_c, "weight", None))
            if weight is not None:
                weight = weight.data.clone()
                bias = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(mod_c, "bias", None))
                bias_data = bias.data.clone() if bias is not None else None
                cls_c = mod_c.__class__.__name__
                base_cls = base_c.__class__.__name__
                if (
                    hasattr(mod_c, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or "Conv1D" in cls_c
                    or "Conv1D" in base_cls
                    or (weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[0] < weight.shape[1])
                ):
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split = weight.shape[0] // 3
                extracted["q"] = (
                    weight[:split],
                    bias_data[:split] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    weight[split : 2 * split],
                    bias_data[split : 2 * split] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    weight[2 * split :],
                    bias_data[2 * split :] if bias_data is not None else None,
                )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("GPT-2 c_attn extraction failed: %s", e)

    if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
        try:
            mod_o = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(mod_o, "base_layer", mod_o)
            w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(mod_o, "weight", None))
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                cls_o = mod_o.__class__.__name__
                base_cls_o = base_o.__class__.__name__
                if (
                    hasattr(mod_o, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                    or "Conv1D" in cls_o
                    or "Conv1D" in base_cls_o
                    or (w_o.dim() == 2 and w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1])
                ):
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                b_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(mod_o, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (
                    w_o,
                    b_o.data.clone() if b_o is not None else None,
                )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("GPT-2 c_proj extraction failed: %s", e)

    # --- 3. BERT / RoBERTa nested ---
    if hasattr(src, "self") and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v")):
        try:
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query") and extracted["q"][0] is None:
                extracted["q"] = (
                    s.query.weight.data.clone(),
                    s.query.bias.data.clone() if s.query.bias is not None else None,
                )
            if hasattr(s, "key") and extracted["k"][0] is None:
                extracted["k"] = (
                    s.key.weight.data.clone(),
                    s.key.bias.data.clone() if s.key.bias is not None else None,
                )
            if hasattr(s, "value") and extracted["v"][0] is None:
                extracted["v"] = (
                    s.value.weight.data.clone(),
                    s.value.bias.data.clone() if s.value.bias is not None else None,
                )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("Nested self extraction failed: %s", e)

    if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
        try:
            dense = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (
                dense.weight.data.clone(),
                dense.bias.data.clone() if dense.bias is not None else None,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("output.dense extraction failed: %s", e)

    # --- 4. Flat modules ---
    named_map = {
        "q": ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"),
        "k": ("k_proj", "k_lin", "key"),
        "v": ("v_proj", "v_lin", "value"),
        "o": ("o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"),
    }
    for proj, names in named_map.items():
        if extracted[proj][0] is None:
            for name in names:
                if hasattr(src, name):
                    try:
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        if hasattr(layer, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                layer.weight.data.clone(),
                                (
                                    layer.bias.data.clone()
                                    if getattr(layer, "bias", None) is not None
                                    else None
                                ),
                            )
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        continue

    # --- 5. Nested encoder detection (MDP-FIX-05) ---
    cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
    has_out_proj = any(
        hasattr(src, a)
        for a in (
            "dense",
            "out_proj",
            "c_proj",
            "out_lin",
            "o_proj",
            "wo",
            "o",
            "proj",
        )
    )
    is_nested = False
    if (
        hasattr(src, "self")
        and hasattr(src.self, "query")
        or cls_name
        in (
            "BertSelfAttention",
            "RobertaSelfAttention",
            "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
            "ElectraSelfAttention",
        )
    ):
        is_nested = True
    elif cls_name in ("DistilBertAttention", "DistilBertSABlock"):
        # DistilBERT nests attention inside a container but has an out_lin
        is_nested = True
    elif (
        cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention")
        and not has_out_proj
        or cls_name.endswith("Attention")
        and not has_out_proj
    ):
        is_nested = True
    self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

    # --- 6. Missing check ---
    missing = [k for k in ("q", "k", "v") if extracted[k][0] is None]
    if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
        missing.append("o")
    if missing:
        logger.warning("MoEDeltaKVPaged: missing %s; aborting copy.", missing)
        return

    # --- 7. Storage tracking ---
    try:
        for p in src.parameters():
            self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # --- 8. Copy into projections ---
    with torch.no_grad():
        # Q, K, V
        for proj_key, target in (
            ("q", self.query),
            ("k", self.key),
            ("v", self.value),
        ):
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is None:
                continue
            in_dim = self.hidden_size
            out_dim = target.weight.shape[0]

            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                w = w.t().contiguous()

            # GQA expansion (repeat)
            if w.shape[0] < out_dim and out_dim % w.shape[0] == 0:
                rep = out_dim // w.shape[0]
                w = w.repeat(rep, 1)
                if b is not None:
                    b = b.repeat(rep)
            elif w.shape[0] > out_dim and w.shape[0] % out_dim == 0:
                rep = w.shape[0] // out_dim
                w = w.view(out_dim, rep, -1).mean(dim=1)
                if b is not None:
                    b = b.view(out_dim, rep).mean(dim=1)

            target.weight.data.zero_()
            out_rows = min(out_dim, w.shape[0])
            in_cols = min(in_dim, w.shape[1])
            target.weight.data[:out_rows, :in_cols].copy_(
                w[:out_rows, :in_cols].to(target.weight.device, target.weight.dtype)
            )
            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                mb = min(target.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                target.bias.data.zero_()
                target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.device, target.bias.dtype))

        # Output projection
        w_o, b_o = extracted["o"]
        if w_o is not None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
            in_dim = self.all_head_size
            out_dim = self.hidden_size
            if w_o.dim() == 2 and w_o.shape[1] != in_dim and w_o.shape[0] == in_dim:
                w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
            self.out_proj.weight.data.zero_()
            out_rows = min(out_dim, w_o.shape[0])
            in_cols = min(in_dim, w_o.shape[1])
            self.out_proj.weight.data[:out_rows, :in_cols].copy_(
                w_o[:out_rows, :in_cols].to(
                    self.out_proj.weight.device, self.out_proj.weight.dtype
                )
            )
            if b_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                mb = min(self.out_proj.bias.numel(), b_o.numel())
                self.out_proj.bias.data.zero_()
                self.out_proj.bias.data[:mb].copy_(
                    b_o[:mb].to(self.out_proj.bias.device, self.out_proj.bias.dtype)
                )
        elif self.is_nested_encoder:
            # Nested encoder: out_proj is bypassed in forward.
            pass

        # Freeze base weights
        for m in (self.query, self.key, self.value, self.out_proj):
            m.freeze_base_weights()

    # --- 9. Storage accounting ---
    try:
        for m in (self.query, self.key, self.value, self.out_proj):
            for p in m.parameters():
                self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # --- 10. is_decoder override ---
    if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
        self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder

    self._source_id = id(src)
def forward(self,
hidden_states: torch.Tensor,

def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool | None = False,
    output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
    return_dict: bool | None = True,
    position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
    *args: Any,
    **kwargs: Any,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:

    if not hidden_states.is_contiguous():
        hidden_states = hidden_states.contiguous()

    B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
    H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
    H_kv = self.num_kv_heads
    device = hidden_states.device
    dtype = hidden_states.dtype

    is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
    kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
    kv_len = kv_input.shape[1]

    # ---- Projections ----
    q = self.query(hidden_states).view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
    k = self.key(kv_input).view(B, kv_len, H_kv, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
    v = self.value(kv_input).view(B, kv_len, H_kv, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

    # ---- RoPE ----
    if position_embeddings is None:
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
    if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
        cos, sin = position_embeddings
        q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

    # ---- GQA ----
    if H_kv != H:
        k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
        v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

    # ---- Delta-KV compression (MDP-FIX-02) ----
    if self.enable_deltakv and kv_len > 1:
        k_hat, v_hat, err_k, err_v = simulate_paged_delta_kv(
            k,
            v,
            rank=self.svd_rank,
            bits=self.q_bits,
            max_delta_error=self.max_delta_error,
        )
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.delta_kv_error_k.copy_(torch.tensor(err_k, device=device))
            self.delta_kv_error_v.copy_(torch.tensor(err_v, device=device))
    else:
        k_hat, v_hat = k, v

    # ---- Mask normalization ----
    valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None
    if attention_mask is not None:
        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                valid_mask = attention_mask
            elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                valid_mask = attention_mask > -1e4
            else:
                valid_mask = attention_mask > 0
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            m = attention_mask
            valid_mask = (m > -1e4).squeeze(1) if m.dtype.is_floating_point else m.squeeze(1)
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            m = attention_mask
            v_bool = (
                m
                if m.dtype == torch.bool
                else (m > -1e4 if m.dtype.is_floating_point else m > 0)
            )
            valid_mask = v_bool.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
        if valid_mask is not None and valid_mask.shape[-1] != kv_len:
            valid_mask = None

    if valid_mask is None:
        valid_mask = torch.ones(B, kv_len, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

    # ---- Decide execution path ----
    num_pages = (kv_len + self.page_size - 1) // self.page_size
    use_paged = self.enable_moe and num_pages > 1 and not is_cross

    if use_paged:
        context, attn_probs = self._paged_moe_attention(q, k_hat, v_hat, valid_mask)
    else:
        # ---- Dense path ----
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
        scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k_hat.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        if self.is_causal and not is_cross and N > 1:
            diag = 1 + max(0, kv_len - N)
            causal = (
                torch.triu(
                    torch.full(
                        (N, kv_len),
                        float("-inf"),
                        device=device,
                        dtype=torch.float32,
                    ),
                    diagonal=diag,
                )
                .unsqueeze(0)
                .unsqueeze(0)
            )
            scores = scores + causal

        scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask[:, None, None, :], -1e4)
        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        attn_probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
        attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)
        context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, v_hat)

        with torch.no_grad():
            self.aux_loss.zero_()

    # ---- head_mask ----
    if head_mask is not None:
        hm = head_mask.to(context.dtype)
        if hm.dim() == 1:
            hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 2:
            hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], hm.shape[1], 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 3:
            hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
        context = context * hm

    # ---- Reshape to (B, N, all_head_size) ----
    context = context[:, :, :N, :] if context.shape[2] != N else context
    context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
    context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

    # ---- Output projection ----
    if self.is_nested_encoder:
        out = context
    else:
        out = self.out_proj(context)

    # MDP-FIX-13: always return a 2-tuple with attn = tensor or None
    attn_ret = attn_probs if output_attentions else None
    return out, attn_ret

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.
