@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("padre_attention")
class PADReAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Polynomial Attention with Dropout Regularization (PADRe).
    Replaces softmax with centered, numerically stable polynomial kernel (1 + S/order)^order.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        order: int = 2,
        temperature: float = 1.0,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank=order)
        self.config = config
        self.comp_config = comp_config or _cfg_get(config, "compression_config", None)

        self.hidden_size = _cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        if (
            (config is None or not hasattr(config, "hidden_size"))
            and "hidden_size" not in kwargs
            and pretrained_attn is not None
        ):
            for cand in ["q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "c_attn"]:
                mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, cand, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    w = mod.weight
                    self.hidden_size = w.shape[1] if w.ndim == 2 else self.hidden_size
                    break

        self.num_heads = _cfg_get(
            config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
        )
        self.head_dim = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "head_dim",
            kwargs.get(
                "head_dim",
                self.hidden_size // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64,
            ),
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.order = _cfg_get(
            self.comp_config,
            "padre_order",
            _cfg_get(config, "padre_order", kwargs.get("order", order)),
        )
        self.temperature = _cfg_get(
            self.comp_config,
            "padre_temperature",
            _cfg_get(config, "padre_temperature", kwargs.get("temperature", temperature)),
        )
        self.max_error = float(
            _cfg_get(self.comp_config, "max_error", kwargs.get("max_error", 0.20))
        )

        # Causal decoder detection
        is_dec = _cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        model_type = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "")).lower()
        _mt = (model_type or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False
        self.effective_parameter_ratio = 1.0

        # LoRA config
        self.lora_rank = _cfg_get(
            self.comp_config,
            "lora_rank",
            _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
        )
        self.lora_alpha = _cfg_get(
            self.comp_config,
            "lora_alpha",
            _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # Aliases
        self.query = self.q_proj
        self.key = self.k_proj
        self.value = self.v_proj

        dropout_p = _cfg_get(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", kwargs.get("dropout", 0.1))
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {
            "order": self.order,
            "temperature": self.temperature,
            "lora_rank": self.lora_rank,
            "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
            "num_heads": self.num_heads,
            "num_kv_heads": self.num_kv_heads,
            "is_decoder": self.is_decoder,
            "is_nested_encoder": self.is_nested_encoder,
            "effective_parameter_ratio": self.effective_parameter_ratio,
        }

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        """
        Robust weight copying across GPT-2, RoBERTa/BERT nested, Qwen/LLaMA, and DistilBERT.
        Validates Frobenius norm >= 1e-4.
        """
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        proj_candidates = {
            "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
            "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
            "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
            "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa nested encoder
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None:
                    base_m = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                    w = getattr(base_m, "weight", getattr(mod, "weight", None))
                    b = getattr(base_m, "bias", getattr(mod, "bias", None))
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[proj_key] = (
                            w.data.clone(),
                            b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                        )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                base_o = getattr(out_mod, "base_layer", out_mod)
                w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(out_mod, "weight", None))
                b = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(out_mod, "bias", None))
                if w is not None:
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        w.data.clone(),
                        b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                    )

        # 3. Flat modules (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None:
                            base_m = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                            w = getattr(base_m, "weight", getattr(mod, "weight", None))
                            b = getattr(base_m, "bias", getattr(mod, "bias", None))
                            if w is not None:
                                extracted[proj_key] = (
                                    w.data.clone(),
                                    b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                                )
                                break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                "PADReAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections %s from %s.",
                missing_after,
                src.__class__.__name__,
            )
            return None

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        # Check source for LoRA adapters to preserve if present
        source_lora: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {}
        for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
            mod = None
            if proj_key == "o" and hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                mod = src.output.dense
            elif hasattr(src, "self"):
                sub_map = {"q": "query", "k": "key", "v": "value"}
                mod = getattr(src.self, sub_map.get(proj_key, ""), None)
            else:
                for name in proj_candidates.get(proj_key, []):
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        break
            if mod is not None:
                lA = getattr(mod, "lora_A", None)
                lB = getattr(mod, "lora_B", None)
                if lA is not None and lB is not None:
                    # In some implementations lora_A is a Parameter, in PEFT it's a ModuleDict/Linear
                    tA = getattr(lA, "weight", lA)
                    tB = getattr(lB, "weight", lB)
                    if isinstance(tA, (torch.Tensor, nn.Parameter)) and isinstance(
                        tB, (torch.Tensor, nn.Parameter)
                    ):
                        source_lora[proj_key] = (tA.data.clone(), tB.data.clone())

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue

                src_norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item()
                if src_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"Source weight for {proj_key} has near-zero norm {src_norm:.6f} < 1e-4"
                    )

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                    and w.shape[1] == target_mod.weight.shape[1]
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "Compressor copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights."
                    )

                # Copy source LoRA adapter weights if compatible
                if (
                    proj_key in source_lora
                    and target_mod.lora_A is not None
                    and target_mod.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    slA, slB = source_lora[proj_key]
                    if slA.shape == target_mod.lora_A.shape:
                        target_mod.lora_A.copy_(slA)
                    if slB.shape == target_mod.lora_B.shape:
                        target_mod.lora_B.copy_(slB)

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

        return True

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        """
        Forward pass with polynomial attention:
        1. Linear projection to Q, K, V
        2. Optional RoPE embedding
        3. GQA head expansion
        4. Scaled polynomial scoring with max-centering for stability
        5. Causal and padding masking
        6. Context readout and projection
        """
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape

        q = (
            self.q_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        k = (
            self.k_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.v_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # Apply RoPE if provided
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # GQA head expansion
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

        # Raw scores: (B, H, N, N)
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        # Determine invalid mask for padding and causal
        invalid_mask = torch.zeros(B, 1, N, N, device=scores.device, dtype=torch.bool)
        if attention_mask is not None:
            valid_tok = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, scores.device)  # (B, N) bool
            invalid_pad = ~valid_tok.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)  # (B, 1, 1, N)
            invalid_mask = invalid_mask | invalid_pad

        if self.is_decoder:
            causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(N, N, device=scores.device, dtype=torch.bool))
            invalid_causal = ~causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)  # (1, 1, N, N)
            invalid_mask = invalid_mask | invalid_causal

        # Apply temperature and re-center over valid scores for numerical stability
        fill_val = -1e4 if scores.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16) else -1e9
        masked_for_max = scores.masked_fill(invalid_mask, fill_val)
        max_scores = masked_for_max.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True).values
        # If all keys are masked, avoid large negative value producing NaN
        thresh = -5e3 if scores.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16) else -1e8
        max_scores = torch.where(max_scores <= thresh, torch.zeros_like(max_scores), max_scores)

        scores_centered = (scores - max_scores) / max(self.temperature, 1e-6)

        # Polynomial kernel: (1 + S/order)^order, clamped to avoid negative base or overflow
        base = torch.clamp(1.0 + (scores_centered / self.order), min=0.0, max=10.0)
        attn_weights = base**self.order

        # Zero out invalid positions
        attn_weights = attn_weights.masked_fill(invalid_mask, 0.0)

        # Normalize across key dimension
        denom = attn_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-8
        attn_weights = attn_weights / denom

        if head_mask is not None:
            hm = head_mask.to(scores.device)
            if hm.dim() == 1:
                hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 2:
                hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], hm.shape[1], 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 3:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(-1)
            attn_weights = attn_weights * hm

        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)
        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(context)

        return (output, attn_weights if output_attentions else None)

    def get_ortho_loss(self) -> Any:
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj.weight.device)

Polynomial Attention with Dropout Regularization (PADRe). Replaces softmax with centered, numerically stable polynomial kernel (1 + S/order)^order.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

src: Any) ‑> None

        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]

        logger.warning(
            "PADReAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections %s from %s.",
            missing_after,
            src.__class__.__name__,
        )
        return None

    target_map = {
        "q": self.q_proj,
        "k": self.k_proj,
        "v": self.v_proj,
        "o": self.out_proj,
    }

    # Check source for LoRA adapters to preserve if present
    source_lora: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {}
    for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
        mod = None
        if proj_key == "o" and hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
            mod = src.output.dense
        elif hasattr(src, "self"):
            sub_map = {"q": "query", "k": "key", "v": "value"}
            mod = getattr(src.self, sub_map.get(proj_key, ""), None)

            lA = getattr(mod, "lora_A", None)
            lB = getattr(mod, "lora_B", None)
            if lA is not None and lB is not None:
                # In some implementations lora_A is a Parameter, in PEFT it's a ModuleDict/Linear
                tA = getattr(lA, "weight", lA)
                tB = getattr(lB, "weight", lB)
                if isinstance(tA, (torch.Tensor, nn.Parameter)) and isinstance(
                    tB, (torch.Tensor, nn.Parameter)
                ):
                    source_lora[proj_key] = (tA.data.clone(), tB.data.clone())

    with torch.no_grad():
        for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is None:
                if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                    if target_mod.bias is not None:
                        nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                    continue

            src_norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item()
            if src_norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"Source weight for {proj_key} has near-zero norm {src_norm:.6f} < 1e-4"
                )

            if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
            elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
            elif (
                w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                and w.shape[1] == target_mod.weight.shape[1]
            ):
                repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
            else:
                min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                target_mod.weight.zero_()
                target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

            if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                    target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                elif (
                    b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                    target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                else:
                    min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                    target_mod.bias.zero_()
                    target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
            elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

            fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
            if fnorm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    "Compressor copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights."
                )

            # Copy source LoRA adapter weights if compatible
            if (
                proj_key in source_lora
                and target_mod.lora_A is not None
                and target_mod.lora_B is not None
            ):
                slA, slB = source_lora[proj_key]
                if slA.shape == target_mod.lora_A.shape:
                    target_mod.lora_A.copy_(slA)
                if slB.shape == target_mod.lora_B.shape:
                    target_mod.lora_B.copy_(slB)

    if self.freeze_base:
        for mod in [self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj]:
            mod.freeze_base_weights()

    return True

Robust weight copying across GPT-2, RoBERTa/BERT nested, Qwen/LLaMA, and DistilBERT. Validates Frobenius norm >= 1e-4.

def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool | None = False,
    output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
    return_dict: bool | None = True,
    position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
    *args: Any,
    **kwargs: Any,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
    """
    Forward pass with polynomial attention:
    1. Linear projection to Q, K, V
    2. Optional RoPE embedding
    3. GQA head expansion
    4. Scaled polynomial scoring with max-centering for stability
    5. Causal and padding masking
    6. Context readout and projection
    """
    B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape

    q = (
        self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )
    k = (
        self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )
    v = (
        self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )

    # Apply RoPE if provided
    if position_embeddings is None:
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
    if position_embeddings is not None:
        cos, sin = position_embeddings
        q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

    # GQA head expansion
    if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
        k = repeat_kv(k, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
        v = repeat_kv(v, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

    # Raw scores: (B, H, N, N)
    scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
    scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

    # Determine invalid mask for padding and causal
    invalid_mask = torch.zeros(B, 1, N, N, device=scores.device, dtype=torch.bool)
    if attention_mask is not None:
        valid_tok = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, scores.device)  # (B, N) bool
        invalid_pad = ~valid_tok.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)  # (B, 1, 1, N)
        invalid_mask = invalid_mask | invalid_pad

    if self.is_decoder:
        causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(N, N, device=scores.device, dtype=torch.bool))
        invalid_causal = ~causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)  # (1, 1, N, N)
        invalid_mask = invalid_mask | invalid_causal

    # Apply temperature and re-center over valid scores for numerical stability
    fill_val = -1e4 if scores.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16) else -1e9
    masked_for_max = scores.masked_fill(invalid_mask, fill_val)
    max_scores = masked_for_max.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True).values
    # If all keys are masked, avoid large negative value producing NaN
    thresh = -5e3 if scores.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16) else -1e8
    max_scores = torch.where(max_scores <= thresh, torch.zeros_like(max_scores), max_scores)

    scores_centered = (scores - max_scores) / max(self.temperature, 1e-6)

    # Polynomial kernel: (1 + S/order)^order, clamped to avoid negative base or overflow
    base = torch.clamp(1.0 + (scores_centered / self.order), min=0.0, max=10.0)
    attn_weights = base**self.order

    # Zero out invalid positions
    attn_weights = attn_weights.masked_fill(invalid_mask, 0.0)

    # Normalize across key dimension
    denom = attn_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-8
    attn_weights = attn_weights / denom

    if head_mask is not None:
        hm = head_mask.to(scores.device)
        if hm.dim() == 1:
            hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 2:
            hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], hm.shape[1], 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 3:
            hm = hm.unsqueeze(-1)
        attn_weights = attn_weights * hm

    attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

    context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)
    context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)

    if self.is_nested_encoder:
        output = context
    else:
        output = self.out_proj(context)

    return (output, attn_weights if output_attentions else None)

Forward pass with polynomial attention: 1. Linear projection to Q, K, V 2. Optional RoPE embedding 3. GQA head expansion 4. Scaled polynomial scoring with max-centering for stability 5. Causal and padding masking 6. Context readout and projection

class REAPMoE(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        d_model: int = 768,
        n_experts: int = 2,
        top_k: int = 1,
        noise_std: float = 0.05,
        use_svd: bool = True,
        config: Any | None = None,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        factory_kwargs = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        if config is not None:
            d_model = getattr(config, "hidden_size", getattr(config, "d_model", d_model))
            n_experts = getattr(config, "num_experts", getattr(config, "n_experts", n_experts))
            top_k = getattr(config, "top_k", top_k)

        self.d_model = d_model
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.top_k = min(top_k, n_experts)
        self.noise_std = noise_std

        self.register_buffer("_aux_loss", torch.tensor(0.0), persistent=False)

        self.router_ln = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model, **factory_kwargs)
        self.router = nn.Linear(d_model, n_experts, bias=False, **factory_kwargs)
        nn.init.uniform_(self.router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)

        if use_svd:

            def expert_layer():
                return nn.Sequential(
                    SVDLinear(d_model, d_model, rank=max(1, d_model // 8), **factory_kwargs),
                    nn.GELU(),
                    SVDLinear(d_model, d_model, rank=max(1, d_model // 8), **factory_kwargs),
                )

        else:

            def expert_layer():
                return nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(d_model, d_model, **factory_kwargs),
                    nn.GELU(),
                    nn.Linear(d_model, d_model, **factory_kwargs),
                )

        self.experts = nn.ModuleList([expert_layer() for _ in range(n_experts)])

        for exp in self.experts:
            final_layer = exp[-1]
            if isinstance(final_layer, SVDLinear):
                nn.init.zeros_(final_layer.U)
                nn.init.zeros_(final_layer.S)
                nn.init.zeros_(final_layer.bias)
            elif isinstance(final_layer, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.zeros_(final_layer.weight)
                if final_layer.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(final_layer.bias)

    @property
    def aux_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self._aux_loss

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        B, L, D = x.shape
        target_dtype = x.dtype

        r_dtype = (
            self.router_ln.weight.dtype
            if hasattr(self.router_ln, "weight") and self.router_ln.weight is not None
            else target_dtype
        )
        normed_x = self.router_ln(x.to(r_dtype)).to(target_dtype)
        rw_dtype = (
            self.router.weight.dtype
            if hasattr(self.router, "weight") and self.router.weight is not None
            else target_dtype
        )
        logits = self.router(normed_x.to(rw_dtype)).to(target_dtype)
        if self.training and self.noise_std > 0:
            noise = torch.randn_like(logits) * self.noise_std
            scores = logits + noise
        else:
            scores = logits

        full_probs = F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).to(target_dtype)
        topk_weights, topk_indices = torch.topk(full_probs, self.top_k, dim=-1)
        topk_weights = (topk_weights / topk_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-9)).to(
            target_dtype
        )

        router_probs = F.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1).view(-1, self.n_experts)
        expert_mask = F.one_hot(topk_indices, num_classes=self.n_experts).float()
        expert_assignment = expert_mask.sum(dim=2).clamp(max=1.0)
        density = expert_assignment.view(-1, self.n_experts).mean(dim=0)
        prob_mean = router_probs.mean(dim=0)
        aux_loss = self.n_experts * torch.sum(density * prob_mean)
        self._aux_loss = aux_loss.detach()

        experts_stack = torch.stack([exp(x).to(target_dtype) for exp in self.experts], dim=2)
        topk_expanded = topk_indices.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, D)
        gathered = torch.gather(experts_stack, 2, topk_expanded)
        out = torch.sum(gathered * topk_weights.unsqueeze(-1), dim=2).to(target_dtype)

        return out, aux_loss

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    B, L, D = x.shape
    target_dtype = x.dtype

    r_dtype = (
        self.router_ln.weight.dtype
        if hasattr(self.router_ln, "weight") and self.router_ln.weight is not None
        else target_dtype
    )
    normed_x = self.router_ln(x.to(r_dtype)).to(target_dtype)
    rw_dtype = (
        self.router.weight.dtype
        if hasattr(self.router, "weight") and self.router.weight is not None
        else target_dtype
    )
    logits = self.router(normed_x.to(rw_dtype)).to(target_dtype)
    if self.training and self.noise_std > 0:
        noise = torch.randn_like(logits) * self.noise_std
        scores = logits + noise
    else:
        scores = logits

    full_probs = F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).to(target_dtype)
    topk_weights, topk_indices = torch.topk(full_probs, self.top_k, dim=-1)
    topk_weights = (topk_weights / topk_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-9)).to(
        target_dtype
    )

    router_probs = F.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1).view(-1, self.n_experts)
    expert_mask = F.one_hot(topk_indices, num_classes=self.n_experts).float()
    expert_assignment = expert_mask.sum(dim=2).clamp(max=1.0)
    density = expert_assignment.view(-1, self.n_experts).mean(dim=0)
    prob_mean = router_probs.mean(dim=0)
    aux_loss = self.n_experts * torch.sum(density * prob_mean)
    self._aux_loss = aux_loss.detach()

    experts_stack = torch.stack([exp(x).to(target_dtype) for exp in self.experts], dim=2)
    topk_expanded = topk_indices.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, D)
    gathered = torch.gather(experts_stack, 2, topk_expanded)
    out = torch.sum(gathered * topk_weights.unsqueeze(-1), dim=2).to(target_dtype)

    return out, aux_loss

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("reap_moe")
class REAPMoEAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        n_experts: int = 2,
        top_k: int = 1,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        pre_ln: bool | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,  # RP-FIX-21
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank or 64)
        self.config = config
        # If comp_config passed, prefer it; else fall back to config attribute.
        if comp_config is None:
            comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None)
        self.comp_config = comp_config

        # RP-FIX-10: strict hidden_size
        h_size = (
            kwargs.get("hidden_size")
            or getattr(config, "hidden_size", None)
            or getattr(config, "d_model", None)
        )
        if h_size is None and pretrained_attn is not None:
            for cand in ("q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "c_attn"):
                mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, cand, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    w = mod.weight
                    if w.ndim == 2:
                        if mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or hasattr(mod, "nf"):
                            h_size = w.shape[0]
                        else:
                            h_size = w.shape[1]
                        break
        if h_size is None:
            raise ValueError("REAPMoEAttention: could not infer hidden_size.")

        self.dim = int(h_size)
        self.num_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            or getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", None)
            or getattr(config, "num_heads", None)
            or 12
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_kv_heads")
            or getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", None)
            or getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", None)
            or self.num_heads
        )
        # RP-FIX-23
        self.num_kv_heads = min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads)
        self.head_dim = (
            kwargs.get("head_dim")
            or getattr(config, "head_dim", None)
            or getattr(config, "d_kv", None)
            or (self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads))
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        # RP-FIX-11
        self.is_decoder = _detect_decoder(config, is_decoder, pretrained_attn)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False  # RP-FIX-22

        # RP-FIX-12
        if pre_ln is None:
            pre_ln = getattr(config, "pre_ln", True) if config is not None else True
        self.pre_ln = bool(pre_ln)

        self.lora_rank = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if config
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and source_attn is not None:
            p = next(source_attn.parameters(), None)
            if p is not None:
                target_dtype = p.dtype

        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )

        # Aliases
        self.q_proj = self.q_lin
        self.k_proj = self.k_lin
        self.v_proj = self.v_lin
        self.out_proj = self.out_lin
        self.query = self.q_lin
        self.key = self.k_lin
        self.value = self.v_lin
        self.dense = self.out_lin
        self.o_proj = self.out_lin
        self.c_proj = self.out_lin

        self.moe = REAPMoE(
            self.dim,
            n_experts=n_experts,
            top_k=top_k,
            config=config,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(self.dim, dtype=target_dtype)
        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(self.dim, dtype=target_dtype)

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self.register_buffer("_attn_aux_loss", torch.tensor(0.0), persistent=False)

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

        if target_dtype is not None:
            self.to(target_dtype)

    @property
    def aux_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.moe.aux_loss

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # RoBERTa / BERT nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # Flat modules
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"REAPMoEAttention.copy_weights: missing mandatory projections "
                f"{missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_lin,
            "k": self.k_lin,
            "v": self.v_lin,
            "o": self.out_lin,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        # For nested, out_lin will be Identity (set below).
                        continue
                    else:
                        raise RuntimeError(f"REAPMoEAttention: missing weight for {proj_key}.")

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                    and w.shape[1] == target_mod.weight.shape[1]
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item())
                if norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "REAPMoEAttention: weight for '%s' has near-zero Frobenius norm (%.2e).",
                        proj_key,
                        norm,
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

        # RP-FIX-16: nested encoder should NOT apply its own out_lin.
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            self.out_lin = nn.Identity()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Mask helper (RP-FIX-20)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _prepare_valid_mask(
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
        B: int,
        N_kv: int,
        device: torch.device,
    ) -> torch.Tensor | None:
        if attention_mask is None:
            return None
        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                return attention_mask
            if attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return attention_mask > -1e4
            return attention_mask > 0
        if attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            # (B, N_q, N_kv) or (B, 1, N_kv)
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                v = attention_mask
            elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                v = attention_mask > -1e4
            else:
                v = attention_mask > 0
            # Reduce over query dimension if present, else keep as is.
            if v.shape[1] > 1:
                v = v.any(dim=1)  # (B, N_kv)
            else:
                v = v.squeeze(1)  # (B, N_kv)
            return v
        if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                v = attention_mask
            elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                v = attention_mask > -1e4
            else:
                v = attention_mask > 0
            return v.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported attention_mask dim: {attention_mask.dim()}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        # RP-FIX-24
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("REAPMoEAttention does not support cross-attention.")

        B, L, _ = hidden_states.shape
        device = hidden_states.device

        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        # Pre-LN: apply norm before attention (RP-FIX-17)
        if self.pre_ln:
            n1_dtype = (
                self.norm1.weight.dtype
                if hasattr(self.norm1, "weight") and self.norm1.weight is not None
                else target_dtype
            )
            h_norm = self.norm1(hidden_states.to(n1_dtype)).to(target_dtype)
            attn_input = h_norm
            residual = hidden_states
        else:
            attn_input = hidden_states
            residual = hidden_states

        # Projections on attn_input
        q = self.q_lin(attn_input).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        k_new = (
            self.k_lin(attn_input).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )
        v_new = (
            self.v_lin(attn_input).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )

        # RoPE with past_len
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            past_len_rope = 0
            if past_key_value is not None:
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 1:
                    past_len_rope = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
                elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                    # RP-FIX-19: use layer_idx
                    idx = min(layer_idx, len(past_key_value.key_cache) - 1)
                    past_len_rope = past_key_value.key_cache[idx].shape[-2]
            cos = cos[..., past_len_rope : past_len_rope + L, :]
            sin = sin[..., past_len_rope : past_len_rope + L, :]
            q, k_new = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k_new, cos, sin)

        # KV-cache
        past_k = past_v = None
        if past_key_value is not None:
            if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 2:
                past_k, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
            elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                idx = min(layer_idx, len(past_key_value.key_cache) - 1)
                past_k = past_key_value.key_cache[idx]
                past_v = past_key_value.value_cache[idx]

        if past_k is not None:
            K_cat = torch.cat([past_k, k_new], dim=-2)
            V_cat = torch.cat([past_v, v_new], dim=-2)
        else:
            K_cat = k_new
            V_cat = v_new

        if past_key_value is not None and hasattr(past_key_value, "update"):
            past_key_value.update(k_new, v_new, layer_idx)

        N_kv = K_cat.shape[2]
        past_len = N_kv - L if self.is_decoder else 0

        # GQA expansion
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            K_full = repeat_kv(K_cat, repeats)
            V_full = repeat_kv(V_cat, repeats)
        else:
            K_full = K_cat
            V_full = V_cat

        # RP-FIX-18: disable SDPA if causal and past_len > 0
        use_sdpa = (
            not output_attentions and head_mask is None and not (self.is_decoder and past_len > 0)
        )

        if use_sdpa:
            q_sdp = q.to(torch.float16) if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
            k_sdp = K_full.to(torch.float16) if K_full.dtype == torch.float32 else K_full
            v_sdp = V_full.to(torch.float16) if V_full.dtype == torch.float32 else V_full

            attn_bias_sdp = None
            if attention_mask is not None:
                if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                    attn_bias_sdp = attention_mask.to(q_sdp.dtype)
                else:
                    token_valid = self._prepare_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N_kv, device)
                    if token_valid is not None:
                        attn_bias_sdp = ((token_valid[:, None, None, :].float() - 1.0) * 1e4).to(
                            q_sdp.dtype
                        )

            is_causal_sdp = self.is_decoder and L > 1 and past_len == 0

            attn_out_sdp = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                q_sdp,
                k_sdp,
                v_sdp,
                attn_mask=attn_bias_sdp,
                is_causal=is_causal_sdp,
                dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
            )
            attn_out = (
                attn_out_sdp.to(hidden_states.dtype)
                .transpose(1, 2)
                .contiguous()
                .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
            )
            attn_out = torch.nan_to_num(attn_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            attn_ret = None
        else:
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), K_full.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            token_valid = self._prepare_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N_kv, device)

            if self.is_decoder:
                if past_len > 0:
                    abs_q = (past_len + torch.arange(L, device=device)).unsqueeze(1)
                    abs_k = torch.arange(N_kv, device=device).unsqueeze(0)
                    causal = (abs_k <= abs_q).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                else:
                    causal = (
                        torch.tril(torch.ones(L, N_kv, device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
                        .unsqueeze(0)
                        .unsqueeze(0)
                    )
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal, -1e4)

            if token_valid is not None:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid[:, None, None, :].bool(), -1e4)

            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
            probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
                probs = probs * head_mask.to(probs.dtype)

            attn_out = (
                torch.matmul(probs, V_full)
                .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
                .contiguous()
                .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
            )
            attn_ret = probs if output_attentions else None

        # RP-FIX-16: skip out_lin for nested encoder
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            proj = attn_out
        else:
            proj = self.out_lin(attn_out)

        proj = torch.nan_to_num(proj, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        # Residual + MoE (RP-FIX-17)
        n2_dtype = (
            self.norm2.weight.dtype
            if hasattr(self.norm2, "weight") and self.norm2.weight is not None
            else target_dtype
        )
        if self.pre_ln:
            h1 = (residual + self.dropout(proj.to(target_dtype))).to(target_dtype)
            h1_norm = self.norm2(h1.to(n2_dtype)).to(target_dtype)
            moe_out, _ = self.moe(h1_norm)
            out = (h1 + self.dropout(moe_out.to(target_dtype))).to(target_dtype)
        else:
            # Post-LN: norm after residual
            n1_dtype = (
                self.norm1.weight.dtype
                if hasattr(self.norm1, "weight") and self.norm1.weight is not None
                else target_dtype
            )
            h1 = (residual + self.dropout(proj.to(target_dtype))).to(target_dtype)
            h1 = self.norm1(h1.to(n1_dtype)).to(target_dtype)
            moe_out, _ = self.moe(h1)
            out = (h1 + self.dropout(moe_out.to(target_dtype))).to(target_dtype)
            out = self.norm2(out.to(n2_dtype)).to(target_dtype)

        out = torch.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        return out.to(target_dtype), attn_ret

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.
