class RoutingAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        num_clusters: int = 16,
        n_iters: int = 3,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank or 64)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None) if config else None

        self.dim = int(_cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)))
        self.num_heads = int(
            _cfg_get(config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12))
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = int(
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
            )
        )

        if self.num_heads <= 0 or self.dim % self.num_heads != 0:
            raise ValueError(f"dim={self.dim} not divisible by num_heads={self.num_heads}")

        self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.num_clusters = int(
            _cfg_get(
                comp_config,
                "num_clusters",
                _cfg_get(config, "routing_num_clusters", num_clusters),
            )
        )
        self.n_iters = max(1, min(int(n_iters), 10))

        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            mt = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            self.is_decoder = (
                bool(_cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", False))
                or bool(kwargs.get("is_causal", False))
                or any(
                    mt.startswith(p)
                    for p in (
                        "gpt",
                        "qwen",
                        "llama",
                        "mistral",
                        "gemma",
                        "phi",
                        "opt",
                        "bloom",
                        "falcon",
                        "olmo",
                        "mpt",
                        "stablelm",
                    )
                )
                or (pretrained_attn is not None and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn"))
            )
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = int(
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
        )
        self.proj_rank = int(
            kwargs.get(
                "proj_rank",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "proj_rank", _cfg_get(config, "proj_rank", 64)),
            )
        )

        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        # Factorized projections
        FF = FactorizedLoRALinear
        self.q_proj = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.k_proj = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.v_proj = FF(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = FF(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            rank=self.proj_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            bias=True,
        )

        dropout_p = _cfg_get(
            config, "dropout", _cfg_get(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1)
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _svd_init(w: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor | None, proj: FactorizedLoRALinear) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = w.detach().float()
            if W.ndim != 2:
                W = W.reshape(W.size(0), -1)

            out_t, in_t = proj.out_features, proj.in_features
            if W.shape[0] != out_t or W.shape[1] != in_t:
                padded = torch.zeros(out_t, in_t, dtype=W.dtype, device=W.device)
                r = min(out_t, W.shape[0])
                c = min(in_t, W.shape[1])
                padded[:r, :c] = W[:r, :c]
                W = padded

            try:
                U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("RoutingAttention._svd_init SVD failed: %s", e)
                return

            r = min(proj.rank, U.size(1), Vh.size(0))
            proj.down.weight.data.zero_()
            proj.up.weight.data.zero_()
            proj.down.weight.data[:r, :].copy_(Vh[:r, :].to(proj.down.weight.dtype))
            US = U[:, :r] @ torch.diag(S[:r])
            proj.up.weight.data[:, :r].copy_(US.to(proj.up.weight.dtype))

            if b is not None and proj.up.bias is not None:
                n = min(b.numel(), proj.up.bias.numel())
                proj.up.bias.data[:n].copy_(b[:n].to(proj.up.bias.dtype))
            elif proj.up.bias is not None:
                proj.up.bias.data.zero_()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # Nested / flat
        elif hasattr(src, "self"):
            s = src
            while hasattr(s, "self") and not any(
                hasattr(s, a) for a in ("query", "key", "value", "q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj")
            ):
                s = s.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"RoutingAttention.copy_weights: missing {missing_after} from {cls_name}."
            )

        for proj_key, target in (
            ("q", self.q_proj),
            ("k", self.k_proj),
            ("v", self.v_proj),
            ("o", self.out_proj),
        ):
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is None:
                if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    eye = torch.eye(
                        self.all_head_size,
                        self.all_head_size,
                        device=target.down.weight.device,
                        dtype=target.down.weight.dtype,
                    )
                    self._svd_init(eye, None, target)
                continue

            # GQA resize for k, v
            if proj_key in ("k", "v"):
                out_t = target.out_features
                src_out = w.shape[0]
                if src_out > out_t and src_out % out_t == 0:
                    rep = src_out // out_t
                    w = w.view(out_t, rep, -1).mean(dim=1)
                    if b is not None:
                        b = b.view(out_t, rep).mean(dim=1)
                elif src_out < out_t and out_t % src_out == 0:
                    rep = out_t // src_out
                    w = w.repeat(rep, 1)
                    if b is not None:
                        b = b.repeat(rep)

            self._svd_init(w, b, target)
            target.freeze_base_weights()

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Robust valid-mask helper
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _prepare_valid_mask(
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
        B: int,
        L: int,
        device: torch.device,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        if attention_mask is None:
            return torch.ones(B, L, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)
        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                return attention_mask
            if attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return attention_mask > -1e4
            return attention_mask > 0
        if attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                return attention_mask.squeeze(1)
            if attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return (attention_mask > -1e4).squeeze(1)
            return (attention_mask > 0).squeeze(1)
        if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                v = attention_mask
            elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                v = attention_mask > -1e4
            else:
                v = attention_mask > 0
            return v.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported attention_mask dim: {attention_mask.dim()}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Stable k-means
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _kmeans_assign(feat: torch.Tensor, K: int, n_iters: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Deterministic k-means assignment on (B, H, L, d).
        Uses evenly-spaced initialization + squared-distance expansion.
        """
        B, H, L, d = feat.shape
        K = min(K, L)
        step = max(1, L // K)
        init_idx = torch.arange(0, L, step, device=feat.device)[:K]
        if init_idx.size(0) < K:
            extra = torch.arange(0, K - init_idx.size(0), device=feat.device)
            init_idx = torch.cat([init_idx, extra])

        feat_f = feat.float()
        centroids = feat_f[:, :, init_idx, :].clone()  # (B, H, K, d)
        cluster_ids = torch.zeros(B, H, L, dtype=torch.long, device=feat.device)

        for _ in range(n_iters):
            # (B, H, L, K) via squared-distance expansion
            x_norm = (feat_f**2).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)  # (B, H, L, 1)
            c_norm = (centroids**2).sum(dim=-1).unsqueeze(-2)  # (B, H, 1, K)
            cross = torch.matmul(feat_f, centroids.transpose(-2, -1))  # (B, H, L, K)
            dist = x_norm - 2 * cross + c_norm
            cluster_ids = torch.argmin(dist, dim=-1)

            one_hot = F.one_hot(cluster_ids, num_classes=K).to(feat_f.dtype)
            counts = one_hot.sum(dim=2).clamp(min=1.0).unsqueeze(-1)
            sum_f = torch.einsum("bhlk,bhld->bhkd", one_hot, feat_f)
            centroids = sum_f / counts

        return cluster_ids

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        # --- Projections ---
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
        K = (
            self.k_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, d)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
            .contiguous()
        )
        V = (
            self.v_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, d)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # --- RoPE ---
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            past_len = 0
            if past_key_value is not None:
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 1:
                    past_len = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
                elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                    past_len = past_key_value.key_cache[0].shape[-2]
            cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
            sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        # --- GQA ---
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            K = repeat_kv(K, repeats)
            V = repeat_kv(V, repeats)

        # --- Valid mask ---
        valid_mask_2d = self._prepare_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device)  # (B, L) bool

        # --- Clustering ---
        K_num = min(max(1, self.num_clusters), L)
        joint_feat = F.normalize(Q + K, p=2, dim=-1)  # (B, H, L, d)
        cluster_ids = self._kmeans_assign(joint_feat, K_num, self.n_iters)  # (B, H, L) long

        scale = d**-0.5
        is_causal = self.is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)

        # ===========================================================
        # Sort + Gather + Chunk attention (real O(N²/K))
        # ===========================================================
        # For each cluster k:
        #   1. Use sort-trick to gather cluster members into a compact block
        #   2. Compute attention on the small (max_q × max_k) matrix
        #   3. Scatter the result back to the original positions
        # ===========================================================

        outputs = torch.zeros_like(Q)  # (B, H, L, d)
        arange_L = torch.arange(L, device=device)

        for k in range(K_num):
            q_mask_k = cluster_ids == k  # (B, H, L) bool
            # Keys: cluster members + token 0 (global anchor) ∩ valid
            k_mask_k = q_mask_k | (arange_L.view(1, 1, -1) == 0)
            k_mask_k = k_mask_k & valid_mask_2d.unsqueeze(1)  # (B, H, L)

            # Determine compact block sizes
            max_q = int(q_mask_k.sum(dim=-1).max().item())
            max_k = int(k_mask_k.sum(dim=-1).max().item())

            if max_q == 0 or max_k == 0:
                continue

            # Sort trick: cluster members get score 2L - pos, others get -pos
            # → argsort descending puts cluster members first (ordered by position desc)
            q_score = q_mask_k.to(torch.int64) * (2 * L) - arange_L.view(1, 1, L)
            k_score = k_mask_k.to(torch.int64) * (2 * L) - arange_L.view(1, 1, L)

            q_idx = q_score.argsort(dim=-1, descending=True)[:, :, :max_q]  # (B, H, max_q)
            k_idx = k_score.argsort(dim=-1, descending=True)[:, :, :max_k]  # (B, H, max_k)

            # Padding mask (which entries are real cluster members)
            valid_q = torch.gather(q_mask_k, 2, q_idx)  # (B, H, max_q)
            valid_k = torch.gather(k_mask_k, 2, k_idx)  # (B, H, max_k)

            # Gather Q, K, V for this cluster
            q_idx_exp = q_idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, d)
            k_idx_exp = k_idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, d)

            Q_k = torch.gather(Q, 2, q_idx_exp)  # (B, H, max_q, d)
            K_k = torch.gather(K, 2, k_idx_exp)  # (B, H, max_k, d)
            V_k = torch.gather(V, 2, k_idx_exp)  # (B, H, max_k, d)

            # Compact attention: (B, H, max_q, max_k) — this is O((N/K)²), not O(N²)
            scores_k = torch.matmul(Q_k.float(), K_k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            # Build pair mask
            pair_mask = valid_q.unsqueeze(-1) & valid_k.unsqueeze(-2)

            # Causal mask via ABSOLUTE positions (preserves causality after sort)
            if is_causal:
                abs_pos_q = q_idx.unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, H, max_q, 1)
                abs_pos_k = k_idx.unsqueeze(-2)  # (B, H, 1, max_k)
                causal_mask = abs_pos_q >= abs_pos_k
                pair_mask = pair_mask & causal_mask

            scores_k = scores_k.masked_fill(~pair_mask, -1e4)
            scores_k = torch.nan_to_num(scores_k, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

            attn_k = F.softmax(scores_k, dim=-1).to(dtype)
            attn_k = torch.nan_to_num(attn_k, nan=0.0)
            attn_k = self.dropout(attn_k)

            out_k = torch.matmul(attn_k, V_k)  # (B, H, max_q, d)

            # Zero out padded queries before scattering
            out_k = out_k * valid_q.unsqueeze(-1).to(out_k.dtype)

            # Scatter back (each token belongs to exactly one cluster → safe)
            outputs.scatter_add_(2, q_idx_exp, out_k.to(outputs.dtype))

        # ===========================================================
        # Post-processing
        # ===========================================================

        # head_mask
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                hm = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
            else:
                hm = head_mask
            outputs = outputs * hm.to(outputs.dtype)

        # Zero out invalid query positions
        outputs = outputs * valid_mask_2d.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(outputs.dtype)

        # Reshape
        context = outputs.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            return context, None

        output = self.out_proj(context)
        output = torch.nan_to_num(output, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        return output, None

class SVDLinear (in_features: int,

Expand source code 
class SVDLinear(nn.Module):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int | None = None,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
        freeze: bool = False,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        factory_kwargs = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.rank = rank or max(1, min(in_features, out_features) // 4)

        self.U = nn.Parameter(torch.randn((out_features, self.rank), **factory_kwargs) * 0.02)
        self.S = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.rank, **factory_kwargs))
        self.V = nn.Parameter(torch.randn((in_features, self.rank), **factory_kwargs) * 0.02)
        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_features, **factory_kwargs))

        if freeze:
            self.U.requires_grad = False
            self.S.requires_grad = False
            self.V.requires_grad = False
            self.bias.requires_grad = False

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return (self.U * self.S.unsqueeze(0)) @ self.V.t().contiguous()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        target_dtype = x.dtype
        V = self.V.to(target_dtype) if self.V.dtype != target_dtype else self.V
        S = self.S.to(target_dtype) if self.S.dtype != target_dtype else self.S
        U = self.U.to(target_dtype) if self.U.dtype != target_dtype else self.U
        b = (
            self.bias.to(target_dtype)
            if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != target_dtype)
            else self.bias
        )
        out = (x @ V) * S @ U.t().contiguous()
        if b is not None:
            out = out + b
        return out.to(target_dtype)

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

@property
def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
    return (self.U * self.S.unsqueeze(0)) @ self.V.t().contiguous()

Methods

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    target_dtype = x.dtype
    V = self.V.to(target_dtype) if self.V.dtype != target_dtype else self.V
    S = self.S.to(target_dtype) if self.S.dtype != target_dtype else self.S
    U = self.U.to(target_dtype) if self.U.dtype != target_dtype else self.U
    b = (
        self.bias.to(target_dtype)
        if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != target_dtype)
        else self.bias
    )
    out = (x @ V) * S @ U.t().contiguous()
    if b is not None:
        out = out + b
    return out.to(target_dtype)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class UnifiedMoE (dim: int,
num_experts: int = 2,
top_k: int = 1,
output_dim: int | None = None,

Expand source code 
class UnifiedMoE(nn.Module):
    """
    Compact MoE with top-k gating.

    BAM-FIX-02 / FIX-12: expert hidden dim is `dim // (num_experts * 4)`,
    output dim is configurable (default `head_dim`, small).
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        dim: int,
        num_experts: int = 2,
        top_k: int = 1,
        output_dim: int | None = None,
        noise_std: float = 0.1,
        expert_hidden_mult: float = 0.25,
        gate_init: float = -3.0,  # BAM-FIX-11
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = int(dim)
        self.num_experts = max(1, int(num_experts))
        self.top_k = min(max(1, int(top_k)), self.num_experts)
        # BAM-FIX-02: default to dim // 4 for compact output
        self.out_dim = int(output_dim) if output_dim is not None else max(16, self.dim // 4)
        self.noise_std = float(noise_std)

        self.router_ln = nn.LayerNorm(self.dim)
        self.router = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.num_experts, bias=False)
        nn.init.uniform_(self.router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)

        hidden_dim = max(16, int(self.out_dim * expert_hidden_mult))
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList(
            [
                nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(self.dim, hidden_dim),
                    nn.GELU(),
                    nn.Linear(hidden_dim, self.out_dim),
                )
                for _ in range(self.num_experts)
            ]
        )

        for exp in self.experts:
            nn.init.normal_(exp[2].weight, std=0.02)
            if exp[2].bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(exp[2].bias)

        # BAM-FIX-11: configurable gate init
        self.gate_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor([float(gate_init)]))

        # BAM-FIX-04: aux loss is an attribute, not a tensor
        self._aux_loss: torch.Tensor | None = None

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, L, D = x.shape
        x_norm = self.router_ln(x)
        logits = self.router(x_norm)

        if self.training and self.noise_std > 0:
            logits = logits + torch.randn_like(logits) * self.noise_std

        all_probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
        topk_scores, topk_idx = torch.topk(all_probs, self.top_k, dim=-1)
        scores = topk_scores / topk_scores.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-6)
        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=0.0)

        # Load balancing aux loss
        if self.training and self.num_experts > 1:
            router_probs = all_probs.view(-1, self.num_experts)
            expert_mask = F.one_hot(topk_idx, num_classes=self.num_experts).float()
            density = expert_mask.sum(dim=2).view(-1, self.num_experts).mean(dim=0)
            prob_mean = router_probs.mean(dim=0)
            self._aux_loss = self.num_experts * torch.sum(density * prob_mean)
        else:
            self._aux_loss = None

        experts_out = torch.stack([exp(x) for exp in self.experts], dim=2)
        topk_out = torch.gather(
            experts_out,
            2,
            topk_idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(B, L, self.top_k, self.out_dim),
        )
        out = torch.sum(topk_out * scores.unsqueeze(-1), dim=2)
        out = torch.clamp(torch.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0), -65000.0, 65000.0)
        gate = torch.sigmoid(self.gate_bias).to(x.dtype)
        return (out * gate).to(x.dtype)

    def get_aux_loss(self, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
        """BAM-FIX-04: return aux loss on the correct device."""
        if self._aux_loss is None:
            return torch.zeros((), device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        return self._aux_loss.to(device)

class UniversalAdapter
Expand source code 
class UniversalAdapter:
    """Universal adapter supporting BERT, DistilBERT, RoBERTa, LLaMA, GPT2, DeBERTa, etc."""

    @staticmethod
    def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any, proj: str) -> tuple:
        """Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

        Handles:
        - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module
        - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly
        - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj
        - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin
        - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight
        - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]
        """
        target = getattr(old_attn, "self", old_attn)
        candidate_names = CANONICAL_PROJECTION_ALIASES.get(proj, [])

        def _unwrap(mod: Any) -> Any:
            """Unwrap LoRALinear / merged adapter layers to their base_layer."""
            if mod is None:
                return mod
            # LoRALinear stores the original frozen layer as base_layer
            if hasattr(mod, "base_layer") and hasattr(mod.base_layer, "weight"):
                return mod.base_layer
            return mod

        def _extract(mod: Any):
            """Return (weight_data, bias) from a module, or None if not a valid weight-holder."""
            mod = _unwrap(mod)
            if mod is None:
                return None
            if not hasattr(mod, "weight") or mod.weight is None:
                return None
            import torch
            import torch.nn as nn

            w = mod.weight
            # Must be either an nn.Parameter or a plain Tensor (Conv1D)
            if not (isinstance(w, nn.Parameter) or isinstance(w, torch.Tensor)):
                return None
            w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
            # Projections must strictly be 2D weight matrices (excludes 1D norms, 3D convolutions, etc.)
            if w.ndim != 2:
                return None
            # Conv1D stores weights transposed: [in_features, out_features]
            is_conv1d = (mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D") or hasattr(mod, "nf")
            if is_conv1d or (w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]):
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            bias = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
            bias = bias.data if (bias is not None and hasattr(bias, "data")) else bias
            # Qwen3.5 Gated Q: query is fused with gate (dim 0 has 2 * num_heads * head_dim)
            if proj == "q" and (
                hasattr(old_attn, "q_norm")
                or old_attn.__class__.__name__ == "Qwen3_5Attention"
                or hasattr(old_attn, "k_norm")
            ):
                cfg = getattr(old_attn, "config", None)
                tc = getattr(cfg, "text_config", cfg)
                h_dim = getattr(tc, "head_dim", 256)
                n_heads = getattr(tc, "num_attention_heads", getattr(old_attn, "num_heads", 8))
                if w.shape[0] == n_heads * h_dim * 2:
                    w = (
                        w.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim, -1)[:, 0, :, :]
                        .reshape(n_heads * h_dim, -1)
                        .contiguous()
                    )
                    if bias is not None:
                        bias = (
                            bias.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim)[:, 0, :]
                            .reshape(n_heads * h_dim)
                            .contiguous()
                        )
            return w, bias

        # 1. Search in target (old_attn.self or old_attn)
        for name in candidate_names:
            if hasattr(target, name):
                result = _extract(getattr(target, name))
                if result is not None:
                    return result

        # 2. Search in old_attn.output (BERT / RoBERTa output projection 'o')
        if proj == "o" and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
            out_mod = old_attn.output
            if hasattr(out_mod, "dense"):
                result = _extract(out_mod.dense)
                if result is not None:
                    return result
            result = _extract(out_mod)
            if result is not None:
                return result

        # 3. Search directly in old_attn (catches cases where there's no .self sub-module)
        for name in candidate_names:
            if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                result = _extract(getattr(old_attn, name))
                if result is not None:
                    return result

        # 4. Fallback for Fused GPT-2 c_attn (must precede positional fallback)
        if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
            fused_result = _extract(old_attn.c_attn)
            if fused_result is not None:
                w, b = fused_result
                split_w = w.size(0) // 3
                split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:split_w, :], (b[:split_b] if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :], (
                        b[split_b : 2 * split_b] if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[2 * split_w :, :], (b[2 * split_b :] if b is not None else None)

        # 4b. Fallback for Qwen3.5 / fused in_proj_qkv
        if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "in_proj_qkv"):
            fused_result = _extract(old_attn.in_proj_qkv)
            if fused_result is not None:
                w, b = fused_result
                k_dim = getattr(old_attn, "key_dim", getattr(old_attn, "k_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
                v_dim = getattr(old_attn, "value_dim", getattr(old_attn, "v_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
                q_dim = w.size(0) - k_dim - v_dim if (k_dim + v_dim < w.size(0)) else k_dim
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:q_dim, :], (b[:q_dim] if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :], (
                        b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim] if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :], (
                        b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim] if b is not None else None
                    )

        # 4c. PyTorch nn.MultiheadAttention in_proj_weight / separate proj_weight
        for obj in (target, old_attn):
            if (
                proj in ("q", "k", "v")
                and hasattr(obj, "in_proj_weight")
                and obj.in_proj_weight is not None
            ):
                w = (
                    obj.in_proj_weight.data
                    if hasattr(obj.in_proj_weight, "data")
                    else obj.in_proj_weight
                )
                b = getattr(obj, "in_proj_bias", None)
                b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                split_w = w.size(0) // 3
                split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:split_w, :].clone(), (b[:split_b].clone() if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :].clone(), (
                        b[split_b : 2 * split_b].clone() if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[2 * split_w :, :].clone(), (
                        b[2 * split_b :].clone() if b is not None else None
                    )

            if proj in ("q", "k", "v"):
                attr_w = f"{proj}_proj_weight"
                if hasattr(obj, attr_w) and getattr(obj, attr_w) is not None:
                    w = getattr(obj, attr_w)
                    w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
                    b = getattr(obj, f"{proj}_proj_bias", None)
                    b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                    return w.clone(), (b.clone() if b is not None else None)

        # 5. Positional fallback: scan all Linear children of target in declaration order.
        #    Only valid if at least 3 children exist (the typical pattern for BERT/RoBERTa variants).
        ordered_proj_map = {"q": 0, "k": 1, "v": 2}
        if proj in ordered_proj_map:
            children_with_weight = [
                (n, m) for n, m in target.named_children() if _extract(m) is not None
            ]
            if len(children_with_weight) >= 3:
                idx = ordered_proj_map[proj]
                if idx < len(children_with_weight):
                    return _extract(children_with_weight[idx][1])

        # 6. Fallback for 'o' projection when replacing leaf self-attention without local out_proj
        # (e.g. RoBERTa/BERT RobertaSelfAttention where output.dense resides in the parent container).
        # Returning an Identity matrix ensures:
        #   (a) custom modules don't crash during weight copying, and
        #   (b) context passes through transparently to parent.output.dense without double projection!
        if proj == "o":
            for mod in target.modules():
                if (
                    hasattr(mod, "weight")
                    and isinstance(getattr(mod, "weight", None), torch.Tensor)
                    and mod.weight.ndim == 2
                ):
                    d = mod.weight.shape[0]
                    eye_w = torch.eye(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                    zero_b = torch.zeros(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                    return eye_w, zero_b

        raise ValueError(
            f"Could not extract '{proj}' projection weights from {type(old_attn).__name__}"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def get_rotary_emb(old_attn: Any) -> Any | None:
        """Return rotary embeddings if present."""
        return getattr(old_attn, "rotary_emb", None)

    @staticmethod
    def get_kv_heads(config: Any) -> int:
        """Return number of key-value heads for GQA/MQA."""
        return getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", 12))

def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any,
proj: str) ‑> tuple
Expand source code 
@staticmethod
def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any, proj: str) -> tuple:
    """Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

    Handles:
    - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module
    - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly
    - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj
    - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin
    - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight
    - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]
    """
    target = getattr(old_attn, "self", old_attn)
    candidate_names = CANONICAL_PROJECTION_ALIASES.get(proj, [])

    def _unwrap(mod: Any) -> Any:
        """Unwrap LoRALinear / merged adapter layers to their base_layer."""
        if mod is None:
            return mod
        # LoRALinear stores the original frozen layer as base_layer
        if hasattr(mod, "base_layer") and hasattr(mod.base_layer, "weight"):
            return mod.base_layer
        return mod

    def _extract(mod: Any):
        """Return (weight_data, bias) from a module, or None if not a valid weight-holder."""
        mod = _unwrap(mod)
        if mod is None:
            return None
        if not hasattr(mod, "weight") or mod.weight is None:
            return None
        import torch
        import torch.nn as nn

        w = mod.weight
        # Must be either an nn.Parameter or a plain Tensor (Conv1D)
        if not (isinstance(w, nn.Parameter) or isinstance(w, torch.Tensor)):
            return None
        w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
        # Projections must strictly be 2D weight matrices (excludes 1D norms, 3D convolutions, etc.)
        if w.ndim != 2:
            return None
        # Conv1D stores weights transposed: [in_features, out_features]
        is_conv1d = (mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D") or hasattr(mod, "nf")
        if is_conv1d or (w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]):
            w = w.t().contiguous()
        bias = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
        bias = bias.data if (bias is not None and hasattr(bias, "data")) else bias
        # Qwen3.5 Gated Q: query is fused with gate (dim 0 has 2 * num_heads * head_dim)
        if proj == "q" and (
            hasattr(old_attn, "q_norm")
            or old_attn.__class__.__name__ == "Qwen3_5Attention"
            or hasattr(old_attn, "k_norm")
        ):
            cfg = getattr(old_attn, "config", None)
            tc = getattr(cfg, "text_config", cfg)
            h_dim = getattr(tc, "head_dim", 256)
            n_heads = getattr(tc, "num_attention_heads", getattr(old_attn, "num_heads", 8))
            if w.shape[0] == n_heads * h_dim * 2:
                w = (
                    w.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim, -1)[:, 0, :, :]
                    .reshape(n_heads * h_dim, -1)
                    .contiguous()
                )
                if bias is not None:
                    bias = (
                        bias.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim)[:, 0, :]
                        .reshape(n_heads * h_dim)
                        .contiguous()
                    )
        return w, bias

    # 1. Search in target (old_attn.self or old_attn)
    for name in candidate_names:
        if hasattr(target, name):
            result = _extract(getattr(target, name))
            if result is not None:
                return result

    # 2. Search in old_attn.output (BERT / RoBERTa output projection 'o')
    if proj == "o" and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
        out_mod = old_attn.output
        if hasattr(out_mod, "dense"):
            result = _extract(out_mod.dense)
            if result is not None:
                return result
        result = _extract(out_mod)
        if result is not None:
            return result

    # 3. Search directly in old_attn (catches cases where there's no .self sub-module)
    for name in candidate_names:
        if hasattr(old_attn, name):
            result = _extract(getattr(old_attn, name))
            if result is not None:
                return result

    # 4. Fallback for Fused GPT-2 c_attn (must precede positional fallback)
    if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
        fused_result = _extract(old_attn.c_attn)
        if fused_result is not None:
            w, b = fused_result
            split_w = w.size(0) // 3
            split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:split_w, :], (b[:split_b] if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :], (
                    b[split_b : 2 * split_b] if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[2 * split_w :, :], (b[2 * split_b :] if b is not None else None)

    # 4b. Fallback for Qwen3.5 / fused in_proj_qkv
    if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "in_proj_qkv"):
        fused_result = _extract(old_attn.in_proj_qkv)
        if fused_result is not None:
            w, b = fused_result
            k_dim = getattr(old_attn, "key_dim", getattr(old_attn, "k_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
            v_dim = getattr(old_attn, "value_dim", getattr(old_attn, "v_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
            q_dim = w.size(0) - k_dim - v_dim if (k_dim + v_dim < w.size(0)) else k_dim
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:q_dim, :], (b[:q_dim] if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :], (
                    b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim] if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :], (
                    b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim] if b is not None else None
                )

    # 4c. PyTorch nn.MultiheadAttention in_proj_weight / separate proj_weight
    for obj in (target, old_attn):
        if (
            proj in ("q", "k", "v")
            and hasattr(obj, "in_proj_weight")
            and obj.in_proj_weight is not None
        ):
            w = (
                obj.in_proj_weight.data
                if hasattr(obj.in_proj_weight, "data")
                else obj.in_proj_weight
            )
            b = getattr(obj, "in_proj_bias", None)
            b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
            split_w = w.size(0) // 3
            split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:split_w, :].clone(), (b[:split_b].clone() if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :].clone(), (
                    b[split_b : 2 * split_b].clone() if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[2 * split_w :, :].clone(), (
                    b[2 * split_b :].clone() if b is not None else None
                )

        if proj in ("q", "k", "v"):
            attr_w = f"{proj}_proj_weight"
            if hasattr(obj, attr_w) and getattr(obj, attr_w) is not None:
                w = getattr(obj, attr_w)
                w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
                b = getattr(obj, f"{proj}_proj_bias", None)
                b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                return w.clone(), (b.clone() if b is not None else None)

    # 5. Positional fallback: scan all Linear children of target in declaration order.
    #    Only valid if at least 3 children exist (the typical pattern for BERT/RoBERTa variants).
    ordered_proj_map = {"q": 0, "k": 1, "v": 2}
    if proj in ordered_proj_map:
        children_with_weight = [
            (n, m) for n, m in target.named_children() if _extract(m) is not None
        ]
        if len(children_with_weight) >= 3:
            idx = ordered_proj_map[proj]
            if idx < len(children_with_weight):
                return _extract(children_with_weight[idx][1])

    # 6. Fallback for 'o' projection when replacing leaf self-attention without local out_proj
    # (e.g. RoBERTa/BERT RobertaSelfAttention where output.dense resides in the parent container).
    # Returning an Identity matrix ensures:
    #   (a) custom modules don't crash during weight copying, and
    #   (b) context passes through transparently to parent.output.dense without double projection!
    if proj == "o":
        for mod in target.modules():
            if (
                hasattr(mod, "weight")
                and isinstance(getattr(mod, "weight", None), torch.Tensor)
                and mod.weight.ndim == 2
            ):
                d = mod.weight.shape[0]
                eye_w = torch.eye(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                zero_b = torch.zeros(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                return eye_w, zero_b

    raise ValueError(
        f"Could not extract '{proj}' projection weights from {type(old_attn).__name__}"
    )

Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

Handles: - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]
